Implement the following plan:

# Detect External Hook Managers and Warn Users

## Context

PR #355 fixed hook installation to use `git rev-parse --git-path hooks`, which correctly resolves `core.hooksPath` (e.g., Husky's `.husky/_/`). However, Husky regenerates `.husky/_/` on every `npm install` (via the `prepare` script), so Entire's hooks get overwritten.

**Goal:** Detect when an external hook manager is in use, warn the user during `entire enable`, and provide copy-paste instructions for a durable integration.

## New Files

### `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/hook_managers.go`

**Data model:**
```go
type HookManager struct {
    Name       string // "Husky", "Lefthook", "pre-commit", "Overcommit"
    ConfigPath string // relative path that triggered detection (e.g., ".husky/")
    OverwritesHooks bool // true if tool will overwrite Entire's hooks on reinstall
}
```

**Detection function:**
```go
func DetectHookManagers(repoRoot string) []HookManager
```

Detection is filesystem-only (no git commands, no file reads — just `os.Stat`):

| Manager | Signal | `OverwritesHooks` |
|---------|--------|-------------------|
| Husky v9+ | `.husky/_/` directory exists | `true` |
| Lefthook | `lefthook.yml` or `.lefthook.yml` exists | `false` |
| pre-commit | `.pre-commit-config.yaml` exists | `false` |
| Overcommit | `.overcommit.yml` exists | `false` |

Returns a slice (repos can have multiple managers, e.g., Husky + lint-staged config).

**Warning generation:**
```go
func HookManagerWarning(managers []HookManager, cmdPrefix string) string
```

Builds warning text from `buildHookSpecs(cmdPrefix)` via an `extractCommandLine()` helper — this keeps the copy-paste instructions always in sync with actual hook content.

**Public entry point (called from setup.go):**
```go
func CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w io.Writer) []HookManager
```

Calls `paths.RepoRoot()`, `DetectHookManagers()`, writes warning to `w`, returns detected managers.

### `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/hook_managers_test.go`

Tests:
- `TestDetectHookManagers_None` — clean repo
- `TestDetectHookManagers_Husky` — `.husky/_/` present
- `TestDetectHookManagers_Lefthook` — `lefthook.yml` present
- `TestDetectHookManagers_PreCommit` — `.pre-commit-config.yaml` present
- `TestDetectHookManagers_Overcommit` — `.overcommit.yml` present
- `TestDetectHookManagers_Multiple` — multiple managers
- `TestHookManagerWarning_Husky` — verifies Husky warning includes all 4 hook commands
- `TestHookManagerWarning_GitHooksManager` — verifies Category B message
- `TestExtractCommandLine` — unit test for the helper

All tests use `t.Parallel()` + `t.TempDir()`.

## Modified Files

### `cmd/entire/cli/setup.go` — 4 call sites

Add `strategy.CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w)` after each `InstallGitHook()` call:

1. **Line 306** — `runEnableWithStrategy()`
2. **Line 404** — `runEnableInteractive()`
3. **Line 614** — `setupAgentHooksNonInteractive()`
4. **Line 715** — `setupGitHook()` (uses `os.Stderr` since this function has no `w` param)

Pattern:
```go
if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
    return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
}
strategy.CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w) // NEW
```

The warning only prints during `entire enable`, never during EnsureSetup self-healing (which calls `InstallGitHook(true)` directly without `CheckAndWarnHookManagers`).

## Warning Messages

**Husky (overwrites hooks):**
```
Warning: Husky detected (.husky/)

  Husky may overwrite hooks installed by Entire on npm install.
  To make Entire hooks permanent, add these lines to your Husky hook files:

    .husky/prepare-commit-msg:
      entire hooks git prepare-commit-msg "$1" "$2" 2>/dev/null || true

    .husky/commit-msg:
      entire hooks git commit-msg "$1" || exit 1

    .husky/post-commit:
      entire hooks git post-commit 2>/dev/null || true

    .husky/pre-push:
      entire hooks git pre-push "$1" || true
```

**Category B tools (backup/chain works, but may need re-enable):**
```
Note: Lefthook detected (lefthook.yml)

  If Lefthook reinstalls hooks, run 'entire enable' to restore Entire's hooks.
```

## Verification

1. `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci`
2. Manual test: create a temp repo with `.husky/_/` dir, run `entire enable`, verify warning appears
3. Manual test: create a temp repo with `lefthook.yml`, verify note appears
4. Manual test: clean repo without hook managers, verify no warning

    15→// Hook marker used to identify Entire CLI hooks
    16→const entireHookMarker = "Entire CLI hooks"
    17→
    18→const backupSuffix = ".pre-entire"
    19→const chainComment = "# Chain: run pre-existing hook"
    20→
    21→// gitHookNames are the git hooks managed by Entire CLI

    23→
    24→// ManagedGitHookNames returns the list of git hooks managed by Entire CLI.
    25→// This is useful for tests that need to manipulate hooks.

    27→	return gitHookNames
    28→}

    30→// hookSpec defines a git hook's name and content template (without chain call).
    31→type hookSpec struct {
    32→	name    string
    33→	content string
    34→}
    35→
    36→// GetGitDir returns the actual git directory path by delegating to git itself.
    37→// This handles both regular repositories and worktrees, and inherits git's
    38→// security validation for gitdir references.
    39→func GetGitDir() (string, error) {
    40→	return getGitDirInPath(".")
    41→}
    42→
    43→// GetHooksDir returns the active hooks directory path.
    44→// This respects core.hooksPath and correctly resolves to the common hooks
    45→// directory when called from a linked worktree.
    46→func GetHooksDir() (string, error) {
    47→	return getHooksDirInPath(".")
    48→}
    49→
    50→// getGitDirInPath returns the git directory for a repository at the given path.
    51→// It delegates to `git rev-parse --git-dir` to leverage git's own validation.
    52→func getGitDirInPath(dir string) (string, error) {
    53→	ctx := context.Background()
    54→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-dir")
    55→	cmd.Dir = dir
    56→	output, err := cmd.Output()
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		return "", errors.New("not a git repository")
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	gitDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
    62→
    63→	// git rev-parse --git-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
    64→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
    65→	if !filepath.IsAbs(gitDir) {
    66→		gitDir = filepath.Join(dir, gitDir)
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	return filepath.Clean(gitDir), nil
    70→}
    71→
    72→// getHooksDirInPath returns the active hooks directory for a repository at the given path.
    73→// It delegates to `git rev-parse --git-path hooks` so Git resolves:
    74→// - linked-worktree common hooks directory
    75→// - core.hooksPath (relative or absolute)
    76→func getHooksDirInPath(dir string) (string, error) {
    77→	ctx := context.Background()
    78→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-path", "hooks")
    79→	cmd.Dir = dir
    80→	output, err := cmd.Output()
    81→	if err != nil {
    82→		return "", errors.New("not a git repository")
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	hooksDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
    86→	if !filepath.IsAbs(hooksDir) {
    87→		hooksDir = filepath.Join(dir, hooksDir)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	return filepath.Clean(hooksDir), nil
    91→}
    92→
    93→// IsGitHookInstalled checks if all generic Entire CLI hooks are installed.

    96→	if err != nil {
    97→		return false
    98→	}
    99→	return isGitHookInstalledInHooksDir(hooksDir)
   100→}
   101→
   102→// IsGitHookInstalledInDir checks if all Entire CLI hooks are installed in the given repo directory.
   103→// This is useful for tests that need to check hooks without changing the working directory.
   104→func IsGitHookInstalledInDir(repoDir string) bool {
   105→	hooksDir, err := getHooksDirInPath(repoDir)
   106→	if err != nil {
   107→		return false
   108→	}
   109→	return isGitHookInstalledInHooksDir(hooksDir)
   110→}
   111→
   112→// isGitHookInstalledInHooksDir checks if all hooks are installed in the given hooks directory.
   113→func isGitHookInstalledInHooksDir(hooksDir string) bool {
   114→	for _, hook := range gitHookNames {
   115→		hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, hook)
   116→		data, err := os.ReadFile(hookPath) //nolint:gosec // Path is constructed from constants
   117→		if err != nil {
   118→			return false
   119→		}
   120→		if !strings.Contains(string(data), entireHookMarker) {
   121→			return false
   122→		}
   123→	}
   124→	return true
   125→}
   126→
   127→// buildHookSpecs returns the hook specifications for all managed hooks.
   128→func buildHookSpecs(cmdPrefix string) []hookSpec {
   129→	return []hookSpec{
   130→		{
   131→			name: "prepare-commit-msg",
   132→			content: fmt.Sprintf(`#!/bin/sh
   133→# %s
   134→%s hooks git prepare-commit-msg "$1" "$2" 2>/dev/null || true
   135→`, entireHookMarker, cmdPrefix),
   136→		},
   137→		{
   138→			name: "commit-msg",
   139→			content: fmt.Sprintf(`#!/bin/sh
   140→# %s
   141→# Commit-msg hook: strip trailer if no user content (allows aborting empty commits)
   142→%s hooks git commit-msg "$1" || exit 1
   143→`, entireHookMarker, cmdPrefix),
   144→		},
   145→		{
   146→			name: "post-commit",
   147→			content: fmt.Sprintf(`#!/bin/sh
   148→# %s
   149→# Post-commit hook: condense session data if commit has Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   150→%s hooks git post-commit 2>/dev/null || true
   151→`, entireHookMarker, cmdPrefix),
   152→		},
   153→		{
   154→			name: "pre-push",
   155→			content: fmt.Sprintf(`#!/bin/sh
   156→# %s
   157→# Pre-push hook: push session logs alongside user's push
   158→# $1 is the remote name (e.g., "origin")
   159→%s hooks git pre-push "$1" || true
   160→`, entireHookMarker, cmdPrefix),
   161→		},
   162→	}
   163→}
   164→
   165→// InstallGitHook installs generic git hooks that delegate to `entire hook` commands.
   166→// These hooks work with any strategy - the strategy is determined at runtime.
   167→// If silent is true, no output is printed (except backup notifications, which always print).
   168→// Returns the number of hooks that were installed (0 if all already up to date).
   169→func InstallGitHook(silent bool) (int, error) {
   170→	hooksDir, err := GetHooksDir()
   171→	if err != nil {
   172→		return 0, err
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	if err := os.MkdirAll(hooksDir, 0o755); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // Git hooks require executable permissions
   176→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create hooks directory: %w", err)
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	// Determine command prefix based on local_dev setting
   180→	var cmdPrefix string
   181→	if isLocalDev() {
   182→		cmdPrefix = "go run ./cmd/entire/main.go"
   183→	} else {
   184→		cmdPrefix = "entire"
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	specs := buildHookSpecs(cmdPrefix)
   188→	installedCount := 0
   189→
   190→	for _, spec := range specs {
   191→		hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, spec.name)
   192→		backupPath := hookPath + backupSuffix
   193→		backupExists := fileExists(backupPath)
   194→
   195→		// Back up existing non-Entire hooks
   196→		existing, existingErr := os.ReadFile(hookPath) //nolint:gosec // path is controlled
   197→		if existingErr == nil && !strings.Contains(string(existing), entireHookMarker) {
   198→			if !backupExists {
   199→				if err := os.Rename(hookPath, backupPath); err != nil {
   200→					return installedCount, fmt.Errorf("failed to back up %s: %w", spec.name, err)
   201→				}
   202→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Backed up existing %s to %s%s\n", spec.name, spec.name, backupSuffix)
   203→			} else {
   204→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: replacing %s (backup %s%s already exists from a previous install)\n", spec.name, spec.name, backupSuffix)
   205→			}
   206→			backupExists = true
   207→		}
   208→
   209→		// Chain to backup if one exists
   210→		content := spec.content
   211→		if backupExists {
   212→			content = generateChainedContent(spec.content, spec.name)
   213→		}
   214→
   215→		written, err := writeHookFile(hookPath, content)
   216→		if err != nil {
   217→			return installedCount, fmt.Errorf("failed to install %s hook: %w", spec.name, err)
   218→		}
   219→		if written {
   220→			installedCount++
   221→		}
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	if !silent {
   225→		fmt.Println("✓ Installed git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)")
   226→		fmt.Println("  Hooks delegate to the current strategy at runtime")
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	return installedCount, nil
   230→}
   231→
   232→// writeHookFile writes a hook file if it doesn't exist or has different content.
   233→// Returns true if the file was written, false if it already had the same content.
   234→func writeHookFile(path, content string) (bool, error) {
   235→	// Check if file already exists with same content
   236→	existing, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // path is controlled
   237→	if err == nil && string(existing) == content {
   238→		return false, nil // Already up to date
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	// Git hooks must be executable (0o755)
   242→	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(content), 0o755); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // Git hooks require executable permissions
   243→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to write hook file %s: %w", path, err)
   244→	}
   245→	return true, nil
   246→}
   247→
   248→// RemoveGitHook removes all Entire CLI git hooks from the repository.
   249→// If a .pre-entire backup exists, it is restored.
   250→// Returns the number of hooks removed.
   251→func RemoveGitHook() (int, error) {
   252→	hooksDir, err := GetHooksDir()
   253→	if err != nil {
   254→		return 0, err
   255→	}
   256→
   257→	removed := 0
   258→	var removeErrors []string
   259→
   260→	for _, hook := range gitHookNames {
   261→		hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, hook)
   262→		backupPath := hookPath + backupSuffix
   263→
   264→		// Remove the hook if it contains our marker
   265→		data, err := os.ReadFile(hookPath) //nolint:gosec // path is controlled
   266→		hookIsOurs := err == nil && strings.Contains(string(data), entireHookMarker)
   267→		hookExists := err == nil
   268→
   269→		if hookIsOurs {
   270→			if err := os.Remove(hookPath); err != nil {
   271→				removeErrors = append(removeErrors, fmt.Sprintf("%s: %v", hook, err))
   272→				continue
   273→			}
   274→			removed++
   275→		}
   276→
   277→		// Restore .pre-entire backup if it exists
   278→		if fileExists(backupPath) {
   279→			if hookExists && !hookIsOurs {
   280→				// A non-Entire hook is present — don't overwrite it with the backup
   281→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: %s was modified since install; backup %s%s left in place\n", hook, hook, backupSuffix)
   282→			} else {
   283→				if err := os.Rename(backupPath, hookPath); err != nil {
   284→					removeErrors = append(removeErrors, fmt.Sprintf("restore %s%s: %v", hook, backupSuffix, err))
   285→				}
   286→			}
   287→		}
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	if len(removeErrors) > 0 {
   291→		return removed, fmt.Errorf("failed to remove hooks: %s", strings.Join(removeErrors, "; "))
   292→	}
   293→	return removed, nil
   294→}
   295→
   296→// generateChainedContent appends a chain call to the base hook content,
   297→// so the pre-existing hook (backed up to .pre-entire) is called after our hook.
   298→func generateChainedContent(baseContent, hookName string) string {
   299→	return baseContent + fmt.Sprintf(`%s
   300→_entire_hook_dir="$(dirname "$0")"
   301→if [ -x "$_entire_hook_dir/%s%s" ]; then
   302→    "$_entire_hook_dir/%s%s" "$@"
   303→fi
   304→`, chainComment, hookName, backupSuffix, hookName, backupSuffix)
   305→}
   306→
   307→// isLocalDev reads the local_dev setting from .entire/settings.json
   308→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   309→func isLocalDev() bool {
   310→	s, err := settings.Load()
   311→	if err != nil {
   312→		return false
   313→	}
   314→	return s.LocalDev
   315→}
   316→

    67→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {

    70→			// to prevent duplicate error output in main.go

   114→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useProjectSettings, "project", false, "Write settings to .entire/settings.json even if it already exists")
   115→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&agentName, "agent", "", "Agent to setup hooks for (e.g., claude-code). Enables non-interactive mode.")
   116→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&strategyFlag, "strategy", "", "Strategy to use (manual-commit or auto-commit)")
   117→	cmd.Flags().BoolVarP(&forceHooks, "force", "f", false, "Force reinstall hooks (removes existing Entire hooks first)")
   118→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&skipPushSessions, "skip-push-sessions", false, "Disable automatic pushing of session logs on git push")
   119→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&telemetry, "telemetry", true, "Enable anonymous usage analytics")
   120→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // completion is optional, flag is defined above
   121→	cmd.RegisterFlagCompletionFunc("strategy", func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string, _ string) ([]string, cobra.ShellCompDirective) {
   122→		return []string{strategyDisplayManualCommit, strategyDisplayAutoCommit}, cobra.ShellCompDirectiveNoFileComp
   123→	})
   124→
   125→	// Provide a helpful error when --agent is used without a value
   126→	defaultFlagErr := cmd.FlagErrorFunc()
   127→	cmd.SetFlagErrorFunc(func(c *cobra.Command, err error) error {
   128→		var valErr *pflag.ValueRequiredError
   129→		if errors.As(err, &valErr) && valErr.GetSpecifiedName() == "agent" {
   130→			printMissingAgentError(c.ErrOrStderr())
   131→			return NewSilentError(errors.New("missing agent name"))
   132→		}
   133→		return defaultFlagErr(c, err)
   134→	})
   135→
   136→	// Add subcommands for automation/testing
   137→	cmd.AddCommand(newSetupGitHookCmd())
   138→
   139→	return cmd
   140→}
   141→
   142→func newDisableCmd() *cobra.Command {
   143→	var useProjectSettings bool
   144→	var uninstall bool
   145→	var force bool
   146→
   147→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   148→		Use:   "disable",
   149→		Short: "Disable Entire in current project",
   150→		Long: `Disable Entire integrations in the current project.
   151→
   152→By default, this command will disable Entire. Hooks will exit silently and commands will
   153→show a disabled message.
   154→
   155→To completely remove Entire integrations from this repository, use --uninstall:
   156→  - .entire/ directory (settings, logs, metadata)
   157→  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)
   158→  - Session state files (.git/entire-sessions/)
   159→  - Shadow branches (entire/<hash>)
   160→  - Agent hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI)`,
   161→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   162→			if uninstall {
   163→				return runUninstall(cmd.OutOrStdout(), cmd.ErrOrStderr(), force)
   164→			}
   165→			return runDisable(cmd.OutOrStdout(), useProjectSettings)
   166→		},
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useProjectSettings, "project", false, "Update .entire/settings.json instead of .entire/settings.local.json")
   170→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&uninstall, "uninstall", false, "Completely remove Entire from this repository")
   171→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&force, "force", false, "Skip confirmation prompt (use with --uninstall)")
   172→
   173→	return cmd
   174→}
   175→
   176→// isFullyEnabled checks whether Entire is already fully set up.
   177→// Returns whether it's fully enabled, and if so, the agent type display name and config file path.
   178→func isFullyEnabled() (enabled bool, agentDesc string, configPath string) {
   179→	// Check settings exist and Enabled == true
   180→	s, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   181→	if err != nil || !s.Enabled {
   182→		return false, "", ""
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Check any agent hooks installed (not just Claude Code — works with Gemini too)
   186→	installedAgents := GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled()
   187→	if len(installedAgents) == 0 {
   188→		return false, "", ""
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Check git hooks installed
   192→	if !strategy.IsGitHookInstalled() {
   193→		return false, "", ""
   194→	}
   195→
   196→	// Check .entire directory exists
   197→	if !checkEntireDirExists() {
   198→		return false, "", ""
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	// Determine agent description from first installed agent
   202→	desc := string(installedAgents[0]) // fallback to agent name
   203→	if ag, err := agent.Get(installedAgents[0]); err == nil {
   204→		desc = string(ag.Type())
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	// Determine config path - check if local settings exists, otherwise show project settings
   208→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   209→	if err != nil {
   210→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
   211→	}
   212→	configDisplay := configDisplayProject
   213→	localSettingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDirAbs, "settings.local.json")
   214→	if _, err := os.Stat(localSettingsPath); err == nil {
   215→		configDisplay = configDisplayLocal
   216→	}
   217→
   218→	return true, desc, configDisplay
   219→}
   220→
   221→// runEnableWithStrategy enables Entire with a specified strategy (non-interactive).
   222→// The selectedStrategy can be either a display name (manual-commit, auto-commit)
   223→// or an internal name (manual-commit, auto-commit).
   224→func runEnableWithStrategy(w io.Writer, selectedStrategy string, localDev, _, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   225→	// Map the strategy to internal name if it's a display name
   226→	internalStrategy := selectedStrategy
   227→	if mapped, ok := strategyDisplayToInternal[selectedStrategy]; ok {
   228→		internalStrategy = mapped
   229→	}
   230→
   231→	// Validate the strategy exists
   232→	strat, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy)
   233→	if err != nil {
   234→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (use manual-commit or auto-commit)", selectedStrategy)
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	// Detect default agent
   238→	ag := agent.Default()
   239→	agentType := string(agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode)
   240→	if ag != nil {
   241→		agentType = string(ag.Type())
   242→	}
   243→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Agent: %s (use --agent to change)\n\n", agentType)
   244→
   245→	// Setup Claude Code hooks (agent hooks don't depend on settings)
   246→	if _, err := setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks); err != nil {
   247→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup Claude Code hooks: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	// Setup .entire directory
   251→	if _, err := setupEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   252→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   256→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   257→	if err != nil {
   258→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   259→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   260→	}
   261→	// Update the specific fields
   262→	settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   263→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   264→	settings.Enabled = true
   265→
   266→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   267→	if skipPushSessions {
   268→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   269→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   270→		}
   271→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   275→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   276→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   277→		f := false
   278→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	// Determine which settings file to write to
   282→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   283→	if err != nil {
   284→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   285→	}
   286→	shouldUseLocal, showNotification := determineSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   287→
   288→	if showNotification {
   289→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Info: Project settings exist. Saving to settings.local.json instead.")
   290→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Use --project to update the project settings file.")
   291→	}
   292→
   293→	configDisplay := configDisplayProject
   294→	if shouldUseLocal {
   295→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   296→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   297→		}
   298→		configDisplay = configDisplayLocal
   299→	} else {
   300→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   301→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   302→		}
   303→	}
   304→
   305→	// Install git hooks AFTER saving settings (InstallGitHook reads local_dev from settings)
   306→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   307→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   308→	}
   309→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Hooks installed")
   310→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Project configured (%s)\n", configDisplay)
   311→
   312→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements
   313→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   314→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nReady.")
   318→
   319→	return nil
   320→}
   321→
   322→// runEnableInteractive runs the interactive enable flow.
   323→func runEnableInteractive(w io.Writer, localDev, _, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   324→	// Check if already fully enabled — show summary and return early.
   325→	// Skip early return if any configuration flags are set (user wants to reconfigure).
   326→	hasConfigFlags := forceHooks || skipPushSessions || !telemetry || useLocalSettings || useProjectSettings || localDev
   327→	if !hasConfigFlags {
   328→		if fullyEnabled, agentDesc, configPath := isFullyEnabled(); fullyEnabled {
   329→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "Already enabled. Everything looks good.")
   330→			fmt.Fprintln(w)
   331→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Agent: %s\n", agentDesc)
   332→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Config: %s\n", configPath)
   333→			return nil
   334→		}
   335→	}
   336→
   337→	// Detect default agent
   338→	ag := agent.Default()
   339→	agentType := string(agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode)
   340→	if ag != nil {
   341→		agentType = string(ag.Type())
   342→	}
   343→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Agent: %s (use --agent to change)\n\n", agentType)
   344→
   345→	// Setup Claude Code hooks (agent hooks don't depend on settings)
   346→	if _, err := setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks); err != nil {
   347→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup Claude Code hooks: %w", err)
   348→	}
   349→
   350→	// Setup .entire directory
   351→	if _, err := setupEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   352→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   353→	}
   354→
   355→	// Use the default strategy (manual-commit)
   356→	internalStrategy := strategy.DefaultStrategyName
   357→
   358→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   359→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   360→	if err != nil {
   361→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   362→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   363→	}
   364→	// Update the specific fields
   365→	settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   366→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   367→	settings.Enabled = true
   368→
   369→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   370→	if skipPushSessions {
   371→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   372→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   373→		}
   374→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   375→	}
   376→
   377→	// Determine which settings file to write to
   378→	// First run always creates settings.json (no prompt)
   379→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   380→	if err != nil {
   381→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   382→	}
   383→	shouldUseLocal, showNotification := determineSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   384→
   385→	if showNotification {
   386→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Info: Project settings exist. Saving to settings.local.json instead.")
   387→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Use --project to update the project settings file.")
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	// Helper to save settings to the appropriate file
   391→	saveSettings := func() error {
   392→		if shouldUseLocal {
   393→			return SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings)
   394→		}
   395→		return SaveEntireSettings(settings)
   396→	}
   397→
   398→	// Save settings before telemetry prompt so config is persisted even if the user cancels
   399→	if err := saveSettings(); err != nil {
   400→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	// Install git hooks AFTER saving settings (InstallGitHook reads local_dev from settings)
   404→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   405→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   406→	}
   407→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Hooks installed")
   408→
   409→	configDisplay := configDisplayProject
   410→	if shouldUseLocal {
   411→		configDisplay = configDisplayLocal
   412→	}
   413→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Project configured (%s)\n", configDisplay)
   414→
   415→	// Ask about telemetry consent (only if not already asked)
   416→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   417→	if err := promptTelemetryConsent(settings, telemetry); err != nil {
   418→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry consent: %w", err)
   419→	}
   420→	// Save again to persist telemetry choice
   421→	if err := saveSettings(); err != nil {
   422→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   423→	}
   424→
   425→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements
   426→	strat, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy)
   427→	if err != nil {
   428→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get strategy: %w", err)
   429→	}
   430→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   431→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   432→	}
   433→
   434→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nReady.")
   435→
   436→	return nil
   437→}
   438→
   439→// runEnable is a simple enable that just sets the enabled flag (for programmatic use).
   440→func runEnable(w io.Writer) error {
   441→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   442→	if err != nil {
   443→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   444→	}
   445→
   446→	settings.Enabled = true
   447→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   448→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   449→	}
   450→
   451→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now enabled.")
   452→	return nil
   453→}
   454→
   455→func runDisable(w io.Writer, useProjectSettings bool) error {
   456→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   457→	if err != nil {
   458→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   459→	}
   460→
   461→	settings.Enabled = false
   462→
   463→	// If --project flag is specified, always write to project settings
   464→	if useProjectSettings {
   465→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   466→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   467→		}
   468→	} else {
   469→		// Always write to local settings file (create if doesn't exist)
   470→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   471→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   472→		}
   473→	}
   474→
   475→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now disabled.")
   476→	return nil
   477→}
   478→
   479→// DisabledMessage is the message shown when Entire is disabled
   480→const DisabledMessage = "Entire is disabled. Run `entire enable` to re-enable."
   481→
   482→// checkDisabledGuard checks if Entire is disabled and prints a message if so.
   483→// Returns true if the caller should exit (i.e., Entire is disabled).
   484→// On error reading settings, defaults to enabled (returns false).
   485→func checkDisabledGuard(w io.Writer) bool {
   486→	enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   487→	if err != nil {
   488→		// Default to enabled on error
   489→		return false
   490→	}
   491→	if !enabled {
   492→		fmt.Fprintln(w, DisabledMessage)
   493→		return true
   494→	}
   495→	return false
   496→}
   497→
   498→// setupClaudeCodeHook sets up Claude Code hooks.
   499→// This is a convenience wrapper that uses the agent package.
   500→// Returns the number of hooks installed (0 if already installed).
   501→func setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks bool) (int, error) { //nolint:unparam // already present in codebase
   502→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
   503→	if err != nil {
   504→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get claude-code agent: %w", err)
   505→	}
   506→
   507→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   508→	if !ok {
   509→		return 0, errors.New("claude-code agent does not support hooks")
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   513→	if err != nil {
   514→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to install claude-code hooks: %w", err)
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	return count, nil
   518→}
   519→
   520→// printAgentError writes an error message followed by available agents and usage.
   521→func printAgentError(w io.Writer, message string) {
   522→	agents := agent.List()
   523→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s Available agents:\n", message)
   524→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   525→	for _, a := range agents {
   526→		suffix := ""
   527→		if a == agent.DefaultAgentName {
   528→			suffix = "    (default)"
   529→		}
   530→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  %s%s\n", a, suffix)
   531→	}
   532→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   533→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Usage: entire enable --agent <agent-name>")
   534→}
   535→
   536→// printMissingAgentError writes a helpful error listing available agents.
   537→func printMissingAgentError(w io.Writer) {
   538→	printAgentError(w, "Missing agent name.")
   539→}
   540→
   541→// printWrongAgentError writes a helpful error when an unknown agent name is provided.
   542→func printWrongAgentError(w io.Writer, name string) {
   543→	printAgentError(w, fmt.Sprintf("Unknown agent %q.", name))
   544→}
   545→
   546→// setupAgentHooksNonInteractive sets up hooks for a specific agent non-interactively.
   547→// If strategyName is provided, it sets the strategy; otherwise uses default.
   548→func setupAgentHooksNonInteractive(w io.Writer, ag agent.Agent, strategyName string, localDev, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   549→	agentName := ag.Name()
   550→	// Check if agent supports hooks
   551→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   552→	if !ok {
   553→		return fmt.Errorf("agent %s does not support hooks", agentName)
   554→	}
   555→
   556→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Agent: %s\n\n", ag.Type())
   557→
   558→	// Install agent hooks (agent hooks don't depend on settings)
   559→	installedHooks, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   560→	if err != nil {
   561→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install hooks for %s: %w", agentName, err)
   562→	}
   563→
   564→	// Setup .entire directory
   565→	if _, err := setupEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   566→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   567→	}
   568→
   569→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   570→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   571→	if err != nil {
   572→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   573→		settings = &EntireSettings{Strategy: strategy.DefaultStrategyName}
   574→	}
   575→	settings.Enabled = true
   576→	if localDev {
   577→		settings.LocalDev = localDev
   578→	}
   579→
   580→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   581→	if skipPushSessions {
   582→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   583→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   584→		}
   585→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   586→	}
   587→
   588→	// Set strategy if provided
   589→	if strategyName != "" {
   590→		// Map display name to internal name if needed
   591→		internalStrategy := strategyName
   592→		if mapped, ok := strategyDisplayToInternal[strategyName]; ok {
   593→			internalStrategy = mapped
   594→		}
   595→		// Validate the strategy exists
   596→		if _, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy); err != nil {
   597→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (use manual-commit or auto-commit)", strategyName)
   598→		}
   599→		settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   600→	}
   601→
   602→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   603→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   604→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   605→		f := false
   606→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   607→	}
   608→
   609→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   610→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   611→	}
   612→
   613→	// Install git hooks AFTER saving settings (InstallGitHook reads local_dev from settings)
   614→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   615→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   616→	}
   617→
   618→	if installedHooks == 0 {
   619→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Hooks for %s already installed", ag.Description())
   620→		if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
   621→			msg += " (Preview)"
   622→		}
   623→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\n", msg)
   624→	} else {
   625→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Installed %d hooks for %s", installedHooks, ag.Description())
   626→		if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
   627→			msg += " (Preview)"
   628→		}
   629→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\n", msg)
   630→	}
   631→
   632→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Project configured (%s)\n", configDisplayProject)
   633→
   634→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements (creates entire/checkpoints/v1 branch, etc.)
   635→	strat, err := strategy.Get(settings.Strategy)
   636→	if err != nil {
   637→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get strategy: %w", err)
   638→	}
   639→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   640→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   641→	}
   642→
   643→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nReady.")
   644→
   645→	return nil
   646→}
   647→
   648→// validateSetupFlags checks that --local and --project flags are not both specified.
   649→func validateSetupFlags(useLocal, useProject bool) error {
   650→	if useLocal && useProject {
   651→		return errors.New("cannot specify both --project and --local")
   652→	}
   653→	return nil
   654→}
   655→
   656→// determineSettingsTarget decides whether to write to settings.local.json based on:
   657→// - Whether settings.json already exists
   658→// - The --local and --project flags
   659→// Returns (useLocal, showNotification).
   660→func determineSettingsTarget(entireDir string, useLocal, useProject bool) (bool, bool) {
   661→	// Explicit --local flag always uses local settings
   662→	if useLocal {
   663→		return true, false
   664→	}
   665→
   666→	// Explicit --project flag always uses project settings
   667→	if useProject {
   668→		return false, false
   669→	}
   670→
   671→	// No flags specified - check if settings file exists
   672→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, paths.SettingsFileName)
   673→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); err == nil {
   674→		// Settings file exists - auto-redirect to local with notification
   675→		return true, true
   676→	}
   677→
   678→	// Settings file doesn't exist - create it
   679→	return false, false
   680→}
   681→
   682→// setupEntireDirectory creates the .entire directory and gitignore.
   683→// Returns true if the directory was created, false if it already existed.
   684→func setupEntireDirectory() (bool, error) { //nolint:unparam // already present in codebase
   685→	// Get absolute path for the .entire directory
   686→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   687→	if err != nil {
   688→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   689→	}
   690→
   691→	// Check if directory already exists
   692→	created := false
   693→	if _, err := os.Stat(entireDirAbs); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   694→		created = true
   695→	}
   696→
   697→	// Create .entire directory
   698→	//nolint:gosec // G301: Project directory needs standard permissions for git
   699→	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDirAbs, 0o755); err != nil {
   700→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .entire directory: %w", err)
   701→	}
   702→
   703→	// Create/update .gitignore with all required entries
   704→	if err := strategy.EnsureEntireGitignore(); err != nil {
   705→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .gitignore: %w", err)
   706→	}
   707→
   708→	return created, nil
   709→}

   736→func newCurlBashPostInstallCmd() *cobra.Command {
   737→	return &cobra.Command{
   738→		Use:    "curl-bash-post-install",
   739→		Short:  "Post-install tasks for curl|bash installer",
   740→		Hidden: true,
   741→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   742→			w := cmd.OutOrStdout()
   743→			if err := promptShellCompletion(w); err != nil {
   744→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion setup skipped: %v\n", err)
   745→			}
   746→			return nil
   747→		},
   748→	}
   749→}
   750→
   751→// shellCompletionComment is the comment preceding the completion line
   752→const shellCompletionComment = "# Entire CLI shell completion"
   753→
   754→// errUnsupportedShell is returned when the user's shell is not supported for completion.
   755→var errUnsupportedShell = errors.New("unsupported shell")
   756→
   757→// shellCompletionTarget returns the rc file path and completion lines for the
   758→// user's current shell.
   759→func shellCompletionTarget() (shellName, rcFile, completionLine string, err error) {
   760→	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   761→	if err != nil {
   762→		return "", "", "", fmt.Errorf("cannot determine home directory: %w", err)
   763→	}
   764→
   765→	shell := os.Getenv("SHELL")
   766→	switch {
   767→	case strings.Contains(shell, "zsh"):
   768→		return "Zsh",
   769→			filepath.Join(home, ".zshrc"),
   770→			"autoload -Uz compinit && compinit && source <(entire completion zsh)",
   771→			nil
   772→	case strings.Contains(shell, "bash"):
   773→		bashRC := filepath.Join(home, ".bashrc")
   774→		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile")); err == nil {
   775→			bashRC = filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile")
   776→		}
   777→		return "Bash",
   778→			bashRC,
   779→			"source <(entire completion bash)",
   780→			nil
   781→	case strings.Contains(shell, "fish"):
   782→		return "Fish",
   783→			filepath.Join(home, ".config", "fish", "config.fish"),
   784→			"entire completion fish | source",
   785→			nil
   786→	default:
   787→		return "", "", "", errUnsupportedShell
   788→	}
   789→}
   790→
   791→// promptShellCompletion offers to add shell completion to the user's rc file.
   792→// Only prompts if completion is not already configured.
   793→func promptShellCompletion(w io.Writer) error {
   794→	shellName, rcFile, completionLine, err := shellCompletionTarget()
   795→	if err != nil {
   796→		if errors.Is(err, errUnsupportedShell) {
   797→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion not available for your shell. Supported: zsh, bash, fish.\n")
   798→			return nil
   799→		}
   800→		return fmt.Errorf("shell completion: %w", err)
   801→	}
   802→
   803→	if isCompletionConfigured(rcFile) {
   804→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion already configured in %s\n", rcFile)
   805→		return nil
   806→	}
   807→
   808→	var selected string
   809→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   810→		huh.NewGroup(
   811→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   812→				Title(fmt.Sprintf("Enable shell completion? (detected: %s)", shellName)).
   813→				Options(
   814→					huh.NewOption("Yes", "yes"),
   815→					huh.NewOption("No", "no"),
   816→				).
   817→				Value(&selected),
   818→		),
   819→	)
   820→
   821→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   822→		//nolint:nilerr // User cancelled - not a fatal error, just skip
   823→		return nil
   824→	}
   825→
   826→	if selected != "yes" {
   827→		return nil
   828→	}
   829→
   830→	if err := appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine); err != nil {
   831→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update %s: %w", rcFile, err)
   832→	}
   833→
   834→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion added to %s\n", rcFile)
   835→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Restart your shell to activate")
   836→
   837→	return nil
   838→}
   839→
   840→// isCompletionConfigured checks if shell completion is already in the rc file.
   841→func isCompletionConfigured(rcFile string) bool {
   842→	//nolint:gosec // G304: rcFile is constructed from home dir + known filename, not user input
   843→	content, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)
   844→	if err != nil {
   845→		return false // File doesn't exist or can't read, treat as not configured
   846→	}
   847→	return strings.Contains(string(content), "entire completion")
   848→}
   849→
   850→// appendShellCompletion adds the completion line to the rc file.
   851→func appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine string) error {
   852→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(rcFile), 0o700); err != nil {
   853→		return fmt.Errorf("creating directory: %w", err)
   854→	}
   855→	//nolint:gosec // G302: Shell rc files need 0644 for user readability
   856→	f, err := os.OpenFile(rcFile, os.O_APPEND|os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY, 0o644)
   857→	if err != nil {
   858→		return fmt.Errorf("opening file: %w", err)
   859→	}
   860→	defer f.Close()
   861→
   862→	_, err = f.WriteString("\n" + shellCompletionComment + "\n" + completionLine + "\n")
   863→	if err != nil {
   864→		return fmt.Errorf("writing completion: %w", err)
   865→	}
   866→	return nil
   867→}
   868→
   869→// promptTelemetryConsent asks the user if they want to enable telemetry.
   870→// It modifies settings.Telemetry based on the user's choice or flags.
   871→// The caller is responsible for saving settings.
   872→func promptTelemetryConsent(settings *EntireSettings, telemetryFlag bool) error {
   873→	// Handle --telemetry=false flag first (always overrides existing setting)
   874→	if !telemetryFlag {
   875→		f := false
   876→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   877→		return nil
   878→	}
   879→
   880→	// Skip if already asked
   881→	if settings.Telemetry != nil {
   882→		return nil
   883→	}
   884→
   885→	// Skip if env var disables telemetry (record as disabled)
   886→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   887→		f := false
   888→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   889→		return nil
   890→	}
   891→
   892→	consent := true // Default to Yes
   893→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   894→		huh.NewGroup(
   895→			huh.NewConfirm().
   896→				Title("Help improve Entire CLI?").
   897→				Description("Share anonymous usage data. No code or personal info collected.").
   898→				Affirmative("Yes").
   899→				Negative("No").
   900→				Value(&consent),
   901→		),
   902→	)
   903→
   904→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   905→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry prompt: %w", err)
   906→	}
   907→
   908→	settings.Telemetry = &consent
   909→	return nil
   910→}
   911→
   912→// runUninstall completely removes Entire from the repository.
   913→func runUninstall(w, errW io.Writer, force bool) error {
   914→	// Check if we're in a git repository
   915→	if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
   916→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Not a git repository. Nothing to uninstall.")
   917→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
   918→	}
   919→
   920→	// Gather counts for display
   921→	sessionStateCount := countSessionStates()
   922→	shadowBranchCount := countShadowBranches()
   923→	gitHooksInstalled := strategy.IsGitHookInstalled()
   924→	claudeHooksInstalled := checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled()
   925→	geminiHooksInstalled := checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled()
   926→	entireDirExists := checkEntireDirExists()
   927→
   928→	// Check if there's anything to uninstall
   929→	if !entireDirExists && !gitHooksInstalled && sessionStateCount == 0 &&
   930→		shadowBranchCount == 0 && !claudeHooksInstalled && !geminiHooksInstalled {
   931→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is not installed in this repository.")
   932→		return nil
   933→	}
   934→
   935→	// Show confirmation prompt unless --force
   936→	if !force {
   937→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nThis will completely remove Entire from this repository:")
   938→		if entireDirExists {
   939→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - .entire/ directory")
   940→		}
   941→		if gitHooksInstalled {
   942→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)")
   943→		}
   944→		if sessionStateCount > 0 {
   945→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Session state files (%d)\n", sessionStateCount)
   946→		}
   947→		if shadowBranchCount > 0 {
   948→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Shadow branches (%d)\n", shadowBranchCount)
   949→		}
   950→		switch {
   951→		case claudeHooksInstalled && geminiHooksInstalled:
   952→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI)")
   953→		case claudeHooksInstalled:
   954→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Claude Code)")
   955→		case geminiHooksInstalled:
   956→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Gemini CLI)")
   957→		}
   958→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
   959→
   960→		var confirmed bool
   961→		form := NewAccessibleForm(
   962→			huh.NewGroup(
   963→				huh.NewConfirm().
   964→					Title("Are you sure you want to uninstall Entire?").
   965→					Affirmative("Yes, uninstall").
   966→					Negative("Cancel").
   967→					Value(&confirmed),
   968→			),
   969→		)
   970→
   971→		if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   972→			return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
   973→		}
   974→
   975→		if !confirmed {
   976→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "Uninstall cancelled.")
   977→			return nil
   978→		}
   979→	}
   980→
   981→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nUninstalling Entire CLI...")
   982→
   983→	// 1. Remove agent hooks (lowest risk)
   984→	if err := removeAgentHooks(w); err != nil {
   985→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove agent hooks: %v\n", err)
   986→	}
   987→
   988→	// 2. Remove git hooks
   989→	removed, err := strategy.RemoveGitHook()
   990→	if err != nil {
   991→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove git hooks: %v\n", err)
   992→	} else if removed > 0 {
   993→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed git hooks (%d)\n", removed)
   994→	}
   995→
   996→	// 3. Remove session state files
   997→	statesRemoved, err := removeAllSessionStates()
   998→	if err != nil {
   999→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove session states: %v\n", err)
  1000→	} else if statesRemoved > 0 {
  1001→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed session states (%d)\n", statesRemoved)
  1002→	}
  1003→
  1004→	// 4. Remove .entire/ directory
  1005→	if err := removeEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
  1006→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove .entire directory: %v\n", err)
  1007→	} else if entireDirExists {
  1008→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed .entire directory")
  1009→	}
  1010→
  1011→	// 5. Remove shadow branches
  1012→	branchesRemoved, err := removeAllShadowBranches()
  1013→	if err != nil {
  1014→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove shadow branches: %v\n", err)
  1015→	} else if branchesRemoved > 0 {
  1016→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed %d shadow branches\n", branchesRemoved)
  1017→	}
  1018→
  1019→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nEntire CLI uninstalled successfully.")
  1020→	return nil
  1021→}
  1022→
  1023→// countSessionStates returns the number of active session state files.
  1024→func countSessionStates() int {
  1025→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1026→	if err != nil {
  1027→		return 0
  1028→	}
  1029→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1030→	if err != nil {
  1031→		return 0
  1032→	}
  1033→	return len(states)
  1034→}
  1035→
  1036→// countShadowBranches returns the number of shadow branches.
  1037→func countShadowBranches() int {
  1038→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1039→	if err != nil {
  1040→		return 0
  1041→	}
  1042→	return len(branches)
  1043→}
  1044→
  1045→// checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled checks if Claude Code hooks are installed.
  1046→func checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled() bool {
  1047→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
  1048→	if err != nil {
  1049→		return false
  1050→	}
  1051→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
  1052→	if !ok {
  1053→		return false
  1054→	}
  1055→	return hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1056→}
  1057→
  1058→// checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled checks if Gemini CLI hooks are installed.
  1059→func checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled() bool {
  1060→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)
  1061→	if err != nil {
  1062→		return false
  1063→	}
  1064→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
  1065→	if !ok {
  1066→		return false
  1067→	}
  1068→	return hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1069→}
  1070→
  1071→// checkEntireDirExists checks if the .entire directory exists.
  1072→func checkEntireDirExists() bool {
  1073→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
  1074→	if err != nil {
  1075→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
  1076→	}
  1077→	_, err = os.Stat(entireDirAbs)
  1078→	return err == nil
  1079→}
  1080→
  1081→// removeAgentHooks removes hooks from all agents that support hooks.
  1082→func removeAgentHooks(w io.Writer) error {
  1083→	var errs []error
  1084→
  1085→	// Remove Claude Code hooks
  1086→	claudeAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
  1087→	if err == nil {
  1088→		if hookAgent, ok := claudeAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {
  1089→			wasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1090→			if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1091→				errs = append(errs, err)
  1092→			} else if wasInstalled {
  1093→				fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed Claude Code hooks")
  1094→			}
  1095→		}
  1096→	}
  1097→
  1098→	// Remove Gemini CLI hooks
  1099→	geminiAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)
  1100→	if err == nil {
  1101→		if hookAgent, ok := geminiAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {
  1102→			wasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1103→			if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1104→				errs = append(errs, err)
  1105→			} else if wasInstalled {
  1106→				fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed Gemini CLI hooks")
  1107→			}
  1108→		}
  1109→	}
  1110→
  1111→	return errors.Join(errs...)
  1112→}
  1113→
  1114→// removeAllSessionStates removes all session state files and the directory.
  1115→func removeAllSessionStates() (int, error) {
  1116→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1117→	if err != nil {
  1118→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
  1119→	}
  1120→
  1121→	// Count states before removing
  1122→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1123→	if err != nil {
  1124→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
  1125→	}
  1126→	count := len(states)
  1127→
  1128→	// Remove the entire directory
  1129→	if err := store.RemoveAll(); err != nil {
  1130→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session states: %w", err)
  1131→	}
  1132→
  1133→	return count, nil
  1134→}
  1135→
  1136→// removeEntireDirectory removes the .entire directory.
  1137→func removeEntireDirectory() error {
  1138→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
  1139→	if err != nil {
  1140→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
  1141→	}
  1142→	if err := os.RemoveAll(entireDirAbs); err != nil {
  1143→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove .entire directory: %w", err)
  1144→	}
  1145→	return nil
  1146→}
  1147→
  1148→// removeAllShadowBranches removes all shadow branches.
  1149→func removeAllShadowBranches() (int, error) {
  1150→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1151→	if err != nil {
  1152→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list shadow branches: %w", err)
  1153→	}
  1154→	if len(branches) == 0 {
  1155→		return 0, nil
  1156→	}
  1157→	deleted, _, err := strategy.DeleteShadowBranches(branches)
  1158→	return len(deleted), err
  1159→}

    14→// initHooksTestRepo creates a temporary git repository, changes to it, and clears
    15→// the repo root cache. Returns the repo directory path and the hooks directory path.
    16→func initHooksTestRepo(t *testing.T) (string, string) {
    17→	t.Helper()
    18→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
    19→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
    20→
    21→	ctx := context.Background()
    22→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "init")
    23→	cmd.Dir = tmpDir
    24→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
    25→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
    26→	}
    27→	paths.ClearRepoRootCache()
    28→
    29→	return tmpDir, filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".git", "hooks")
    30→}

    32→func TestGetGitDirInPath_RegularRepo(t *testing.T) {
    33→	t.Parallel()
    34→
    35→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
    36→
    37→	ctx := context.Background()
    38→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "init")
    39→	cmd.Dir = tmpDir
    40→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
    41→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)

    43→

    45→	if err != nil {

    47→	}
    48→
    49→	expected := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".git")
    50→
    51→	// Resolve symlinks for comparison (macOS /var -> /private/var)

    53→	if err != nil {
    54→		t.Fatalf("failed to resolve symlinks for result: %v", err)
    55→	}
    56→	expectedResolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(expected)
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		t.Fatalf("failed to resolve symlinks for expected: %v", err)
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	if resultResolved != expectedResolved {
    62→		t.Errorf("expected %s, got %s", expectedResolved, resultResolved)
    63→	}
    64→}

    66→func TestGetGitDirInPath_Worktree(t *testing.T) {
    67→	t.Parallel()
    68→
    69→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
    70→	mainRepo := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "main")
    71→	worktreeDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "worktree")
    72→
    73→	// Initialize main repo
    74→	if err := os.MkdirAll(mainRepo, 0o755); err != nil {
    75→		t.Fatalf("failed to create main repo dir: %v", err)
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	ctx := context.Background()
    79→
    80→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "init")
    81→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo
    82→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
    83→		t.Fatalf("failed to init main repo: %v", err)
    84→	}
    85→
    86→	// Configure git user for the commit
    87→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "config", "user.email", "test@test.com")
    88→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo
    89→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
    90→		t.Fatalf("failed to configure git email: %v", err)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "config", "user.name", "Test User")
    94→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo
    95→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
    96→		t.Fatalf("failed to configure git name: %v", err)
    97→	}
    98→
    99→	// Disable GPG signing for test commits
   100→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "config", "commit.gpgsign", "false")
   101→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo
   102→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   103→		t.Fatalf("failed to configure commit.gpgsign: %v", err)
   104→	}
   105→
   106→	// Create an initial commit (required for worktree)
   107→	testFile := filepath.Join(mainRepo, "test.txt")
   108→	if err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("test"), 0o644); err != nil {
   109→		t.Fatalf("failed to create test file: %v", err)
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "add", ".")
   113→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo
   114→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   115→		t.Fatalf("failed to git add: %v", err)
   116→	}
   117→
   118→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "commit", "-m", "initial")
   119→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo
   120→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   121→		t.Fatalf("failed to git commit: %v", err)
   122→	}
   123→
   124→	// Create a worktree
   125→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "worktree", "add", worktreeDir, "-b", "feature")
   126→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo
   127→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   128→		t.Fatalf("failed to create worktree: %v", err)
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	// Test that getGitDirInPath works in the worktree
   132→	result, err := getGitDirInPath(worktreeDir)
   133→	if err != nil {
   134→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Resolve symlinks for comparison (macOS /var -> /private/var)
   138→	resultResolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(result)
   139→	if err != nil {
   140→		t.Fatalf("failed to resolve symlinks for result: %v", err)
   141→	}
   142→	expectedPrefix, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(filepath.Join(mainRepo, ".git", "worktrees"))
   143→	if err != nil {
   144→		t.Fatalf("failed to resolve symlinks for expected prefix: %v", err)
   145→	}
   146→
   147→	// The git dir for a worktree should be inside main repo's .git/worktrees/
   148→	if !strings.HasPrefix(resultResolved, expectedPrefix) {
   149→		t.Errorf("expected git dir to be under %s, got %s", expectedPrefix, resultResolved)
   150→	}
   151→}

   229→func TestGetHooksDirInPath_CoreHooksPath(t *testing.T) {
   230→	t.Parallel()
   231→
   232→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()

   239→	}

   250→	}

   252→	if filepath.Clean(relativeResult) != filepath.Clean(relativeExpected) {
   253→		t.Errorf("relative core.hooksPath expected %s, got %s", relativeExpected, relativeResult)
   254→	}
   255→
   256→	// Absolute core.hooksPath should be returned unchanged.
   257→	absHooksPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "abs-hooks")
   258→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "config", "core.hooksPath", absHooksPath)
   259→	cmd.Dir = tmpDir

   261→		t.Fatalf("failed to set absolute core.hooksPath: %v", err)
   262→	}
   263→	absoluteResult, err := getHooksDirInPath(tmpDir)
   264→	if err != nil {
   265→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error for absolute hooks path: %v", err)
   266→	}
   267→	if filepath.Clean(absoluteResult) != filepath.Clean(absHooksPath) {
   268→		t.Errorf("absolute core.hooksPath expected %s, got %s", absHooksPath, absoluteResult)
   269→	}
   270→}

   272→func TestInstallGitHook_WorktreeInstallsInCommonHooks(t *testing.T) {
   273→	mainRepo, worktreeDir := initHooksWorktreeRepo(t)
   274→	t.Chdir(worktreeDir)
   275→	paths.ClearRepoRootCache()
   276→
   277→	count, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   278→	if err != nil {
   279→		t.Fatalf("InstallGitHook() in worktree failed: %v", err)
   280→	}
   281→	if count == 0 {
   282→		t.Fatal("InstallGitHook() should install hooks in worktree")
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// Hooks should be installed in common .git/hooks, not in .git/worktrees/<name>/hooks.
   286→	commonHooksDir := filepath.Join(mainRepo, ".git", "hooks")
   287→	for _, hook := range gitHookNames {
   288→		data, readErr := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(commonHooksDir, hook))
   289→		if readErr != nil {
   290→			t.Fatalf("expected common hook %s to exist: %v", hook, readErr)
   291→		}
   292→		if !strings.Contains(string(data), entireHookMarker) {
   293→			t.Errorf("common hook %s should contain Entire marker", hook)
   294→		}
   295→	}
   296→
   297→	ctx := context.Background()
   298→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-dir")
   299→	cmd.Dir = worktreeDir
   300→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   301→	if err != nil {
   302→		t.Fatalf("failed to get worktree git dir: %v", err)
   303→	}
   304→	worktreeGitDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   305→	if !filepath.IsAbs(worktreeGitDir) {
   306→		worktreeGitDir = filepath.Join(worktreeDir, worktreeGitDir)
   307→	}
   308→	for _, hook := range gitHookNames {
   309→		wtHookPath := filepath.Join(worktreeGitDir, "hooks", hook)
   310→		if data, readErr := os.ReadFile(wtHookPath); readErr == nil && strings.Contains(string(data), entireHookMarker) {
   311→			t.Errorf("worktree-local hook %s should not contain Entire marker (should install in common hooks dir)", hook)
   312→		}
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	if !IsGitHookInstalledInDir(worktreeDir) {
   316→		t.Error("IsGitHookInstalledInDir(worktree) should be true after install")
   317→	}
   318→}

   357→	if err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("test"), 0o644); err != nil {
   358→		t.Fatalf("failed to create test file: %v", err)

   362→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo

   367→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "commit", "-m", "initial")

   373→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "worktree", "add", worktreeDir, "-b", "feature")

   440→func TestIsGitSequenceOperation_Worktree(t *testing.T) {
   441→	// Test that detection works in a worktree (git dir is different)
   442→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   443→	mainRepo := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "main")
   444→	worktreeDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "worktree")
   445→
   446→	if err := os.MkdirAll(mainRepo, 0o755); err != nil {
   447→		t.Fatalf("failed to create main repo dir: %v", err)
   448→	}
   449→
   450→	ctx := context.Background()
   451→
   452→	// Initialize main repo with a commit
   453→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "init")
   454→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo
   455→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   456→		t.Fatalf("failed to init main repo: %v", err)
   457→	}
   458→
   459→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "config", "user.email", "test@test.com")
   460→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo
   461→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   462→		t.Fatalf("failed to configure git email: %v", err)
   463→	}
   464→
   465→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "config", "user.name", "Test User")
   466→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo
   467→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   468→		t.Fatalf("failed to configure git name: %v", err)
   469→	}
   470→
   471→	// Disable GPG signing for test commits
   472→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "config", "commit.gpgsign", "false")
   473→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo
   474→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   475→		t.Fatalf("failed to configure commit.gpgsign: %v", err)
   476→	}
   477→
   478→	testFile := filepath.Join(mainRepo, "test.txt")
   479→	if err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("test"), 0o644); err != nil {
   480→		t.Fatalf("failed to create test file: %v", err)
   481→	}
   482→
   483→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "add", ".")
   484→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo
   485→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   486→		t.Fatalf("failed to git add: %v", err)
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "commit", "-m", "initial")
   490→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo
   491→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   492→		t.Fatalf("failed to git commit: %v", err)
   493→	}
   494→
   495→	// Create a worktree
   496→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "worktree", "add", worktreeDir, "-b", "feature")
   497→	cmd.Dir = mainRepo
   498→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   499→		t.Fatalf("failed to create worktree: %v", err)
   500→	}
   501→
   502→	// Change to worktree
   503→	t.Chdir(worktreeDir)
   504→
   505→	// Should not detect sequence operation in clean worktree
   506→	if isGitSequenceOperation() {
   507→		t.Error("isGitSequenceOperation() = true in clean worktree, want false")
   508→	}
   509→
   510→	// Get the worktree's git dir and simulate rebase state there
   511→	cmd = exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-dir")
   512→	cmd.Dir = worktreeDir
   513→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   514→	if err != nil {
   515→		t.Fatalf("failed to get git dir: %v", err)
   516→	}
   517→	gitDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   518→
   519→	rebaseMergeDir := filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-merge")
   520→	if err := os.MkdirAll(rebaseMergeDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   521→		t.Fatalf("failed to create rebase-merge dir in worktree: %v", err)
   522→	}
   523→
   524→	// Now should detect sequence operation
   525→	if !isGitSequenceOperation() {
   526→		t.Error("isGitSequenceOperation() = false in worktree during rebase, want true")
   527→	}
   528→}

   530→func TestInstallGitHook_Idempotent(t *testing.T) {
   531→	_, hooksDir := initHooksTestRepo(t)
   532→
   533→	// First install should install hooks
   534→	firstCount, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   535→	if err != nil {
   536→		t.Fatalf("First InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   537→	}
   538→	if firstCount == 0 {
   539→		t.Error("First InstallGitHook() should install hooks (count > 0)")
   540→	}
   541→
   542→	// Capture hook contents after first install
   543→	firstContents := make(map[string]string)
   544→	for _, hook := range gitHookNames {
   545→		data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(hooksDir, hook))
   546→		if err != nil {
   547→			t.Fatalf("hook %s should exist after install: %v", hook, err)
   548→		}
   549→		firstContents[hook] = string(data)
   550→		if !strings.Contains(string(data), entireHookMarker) {
   551→			t.Errorf("hook %s should contain Entire marker", hook)
   552→		}
   553→	}
   554→
   555→	// Second install should return 0 (all hooks already up to date)
   556→	secondCount, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   557→	if err != nil {
   558→		t.Fatalf("Second InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   559→	}
   560→	if secondCount != 0 {
   561→		t.Errorf("Second InstallGitHook() returned %d, want 0 (hooks unchanged)", secondCount)
   562→	}
   563→
   564→	// Content should be identical after second install
   565→	for _, hook := range gitHookNames {
   566→		data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(hooksDir, hook))
   567→		if err != nil {
   568→			t.Fatalf("hook %s should exist: %v", hook, err)
   569→		}
   570→		if string(data) != firstContents[hook] {
   571→			t.Errorf("hook %s content changed after idempotent reinstall", hook)
   572→		}
   573→	}
   574→}

   576→func TestInstallGitHook_CoreHooksPathRelative(t *testing.T) {
   577→	tmpDir, _ := initHooksTestRepo(t)
   578→	ctx := context.Background()
   579→
   580→	// Simulate Husky-style override: hooks live outside .git/hooks.
   581→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "config", "core.hooksPath", ".husky/_")
   582→	cmd.Dir = tmpDir
   583→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   584→		t.Fatalf("failed to set core.hooksPath: %v", err)
   585→	}
   586→
   587→	count, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   588→	if err != nil {
   589→		t.Fatalf("InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   590→	}
   591→	if count == 0 {
   592→		t.Fatal("InstallGitHook() should install hooks when core.hooksPath is set")
   593→	}
   594→
   595→	configuredHooksDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".husky", "_")
   596→	for _, hook := range gitHookNames {
   597→		hookPath := filepath.Join(configuredHooksDir, hook)
   598→		data, readErr := os.ReadFile(hookPath)
   599→		if readErr != nil {
   600→			t.Fatalf("expected hook %s in core.hooksPath dir: %v", hook, readErr)
   601→		}
   602→		if !strings.Contains(string(data), entireHookMarker) {
   603→			t.Errorf("hook %s in core.hooksPath dir should contain Entire marker", hook)
   604→		}
   605→	}
   606→
   607→	// Ensure we did not incorrectly write Entire hooks into .git/hooks.
   608→	defaultHooksDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".git", "hooks")
   609→	for _, hook := range gitHookNames {
   610→		defaultHookPath := filepath.Join(defaultHooksDir, hook)
   611→		if data, readErr := os.ReadFile(defaultHookPath); readErr == nil && strings.Contains(string(data), entireHookMarker) {
   612→			t.Errorf("default hook %s should not contain Entire marker when core.hooksPath is set", hook)
   613→		}
   614→	}
   615→
   616→	if !IsGitHookInstalledInDir(tmpDir) {
   617→		t.Error("IsGitHookInstalledInDir() should detect hooks installed in core.hooksPath")
   618→	}
   619→}

   621→func TestRemoveGitHook_CoreHooksPathRelative(t *testing.T) {
   622→	tmpDir, _ := initHooksTestRepo(t)
   623→	ctx := context.Background()
   624→
   625→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "config", "core.hooksPath", ".husky/_")
   626→	cmd.Dir = tmpDir
   627→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   628→		t.Fatalf("failed to set core.hooksPath: %v", err)
   629→	}
   630→
   631→	installCount, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   632→	if err != nil {
   633→		t.Fatalf("InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   634→	}
   635→	if installCount == 0 {
   636→		t.Fatal("InstallGitHook() should install hooks before removal test")
   637→	}
   638→
   639→	// Hooks must be installed in core.hooksPath (not .git/hooks).
   640→	configuredHooksDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".husky", "_")
   641→	for _, hook := range gitHookNames {
   642→		hookPath := filepath.Join(configuredHooksDir, hook)
   643→		if _, statErr := os.Stat(hookPath); statErr != nil {
   644→			t.Fatalf("expected hook %s in core.hooksPath before removal: %v", hook, statErr)
   645→		}
   646→	}
   647→
   648→	removeCount, err := RemoveGitHook()
   649→	if err != nil {
   650→		t.Fatalf("RemoveGitHook() error = %v", err)
   651→	}
   652→	if removeCount != installCount {
   653→		t.Errorf("RemoveGitHook() returned %d, want %d", removeCount, installCount)
   654→	}
   655→
   656→	for _, hook := range gitHookNames {
   657→		hookPath := filepath.Join(configuredHooksDir, hook)
   658→		if _, statErr := os.Stat(hookPath); !os.IsNotExist(statErr) {
   659→			t.Errorf("hook file %s should not exist in core.hooksPath after removal", hook)
   660→		}
   661→	}
   662→
   663→	if IsGitHookInstalledInDir(tmpDir) {
   664→		t.Error("IsGitHookInstalledInDir() should be false after removing hooks in core.hooksPath")
   665→	}
   666→}

   668→func TestRemoveGitHook_RemovesInstalledHooks(t *testing.T) {
   669→	tmpDir, _ := initHooksTestRepo(t)
   670→
   671→	// Install hooks first
   672→	installCount, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   673→	if err != nil {
   674→		t.Fatalf("InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   675→	}
   676→	if installCount == 0 {
   677→		t.Fatal("InstallGitHook() should install hooks")
   678→	}
   679→
   680→	// Verify hooks are installed
   681→	if !IsGitHookInstalled() {
   682→		t.Fatal("hooks should be installed before removal test")
   683→	}
   684→
   685→	// Remove hooks
   686→	removeCount, err := RemoveGitHook()
   687→	if err != nil {
   688→		t.Fatalf("RemoveGitHook() error = %v", err)
   689→	}
   690→	if removeCount != installCount {
   691→		t.Errorf("RemoveGitHook() returned %d, want %d (same as installed)", removeCount, installCount)
   692→	}
   693→
   694→	// Verify hooks are removed
   695→	if IsGitHookInstalled() {
   696→		t.Error("hooks should not be installed after removal")
   697→	}
   698→
   699→	// Verify hook files no longer exist
   700→	hooksDir := filepath.Join(tmpDir, ".git", "hooks")
   701→	for _, hookName := range gitHookNames {
   702→		hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, hookName)
   703→		if _, err := os.Stat(hookPath); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
   704→			t.Errorf("hook file %s should not exist after removal", hookName)
   705→		}
   706→	}
   707→}

   762→func TestInstallGitHook_BacksUpCustomHook(t *testing.T) {
   763→	_, hooksDir := initHooksTestRepo(t)
   764→
   765→	// Create a custom prepare-commit-msg hook
   766→	customHookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, "prepare-commit-msg")
   767→	customContent := "#!/bin/sh\necho 'my custom hook'\n"
   768→	if err := os.WriteFile(customHookPath, []byte(customContent), 0o755); err != nil {
   769→		t.Fatalf("failed to create custom hook: %v", err)
   770→	}
   771→
   772→	count, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   773→	if err != nil {
   774→		t.Fatalf("InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   775→	}
   776→	if count == 0 {
   777→		t.Error("InstallGitHook() should install hooks")
   778→	}
   779→
   780→	// Verify custom hook was backed up
   781→	backupPath := customHookPath + backupSuffix
   782→	backupData, err := os.ReadFile(backupPath)
   783→	if err != nil {
   784→		t.Fatalf("backup file should exist at %s: %v", backupPath, err)
   785→	}
   786→	if string(backupData) != customContent {
   787→		t.Errorf("backup content = %q, want %q", string(backupData), customContent)
   788→	}
   789→
   790→	// Verify installed hook has our marker and chain call
   791→	hookData, err := os.ReadFile(customHookPath)
   792→	if err != nil {
   793→		t.Fatalf("hook file should exist: %v", err)
   794→	}
   795→	hookContent := string(hookData)
   796→	if !strings.Contains(hookContent, entireHookMarker) {
   797→		t.Error("installed hook should contain Entire marker")
   798→	}
   799→	if !strings.Contains(hookContent, chainComment) {
   800→		t.Error("installed hook should contain chain call")
   801→	}
   802→	if !strings.Contains(hookContent, "prepare-commit-msg"+backupSuffix) {
   803→		t.Error("chain call should reference the backup file")
   804→	}
   805→}

   807→func TestInstallGitHook_DoesNotOverwriteExistingBackup(t *testing.T) {
   808→	_, hooksDir := initHooksTestRepo(t)
   809→
   810→	// Create a backup file manually (simulating a previous backup)
   811→	firstBackupContent := "#!/bin/sh\necho 'first custom hook'\n"
   812→	backupPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, "prepare-commit-msg"+backupSuffix)
   813→	if err := os.WriteFile(backupPath, []byte(firstBackupContent), 0o755); err != nil {
   814→		t.Fatalf("failed to create backup: %v", err)
   815→	}
   816→
   817→	// Create a second custom hook at the standard path
   818→	secondCustomContent := "#!/bin/sh\necho 'second custom hook'\n"
   819→	hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, "prepare-commit-msg")
   820→	if err := os.WriteFile(hookPath, []byte(secondCustomContent), 0o755); err != nil {
   821→		t.Fatalf("failed to create second custom hook: %v", err)
   822→	}
   823→
   824→	_, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   825→	if err != nil {
   826→		t.Fatalf("InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   827→	}
   828→
   829→	// Verify the original backup was NOT overwritten
   830→	backupData, err := os.ReadFile(backupPath)
   831→	if err != nil {
   832→		t.Fatalf("backup should still exist: %v", err)
   833→	}
   834→	if string(backupData) != firstBackupContent {
   835→		t.Errorf("backup content = %q, want original %q", string(backupData), firstBackupContent)
   836→	}
   837→
   838→	// Verify our hook was installed with chain call
   839→	hookData, err := os.ReadFile(hookPath)
   840→	if err != nil {
   841→		t.Fatalf("hook should exist: %v", err)
   842→	}
   843→	if !strings.Contains(string(hookData), entireHookMarker) {
   844→		t.Error("hook should contain Entire marker")
   845→	}
   846→	if !strings.Contains(string(hookData), chainComment) {
   847→		t.Error("hook should contain chain call since backup exists")
   848→	}
   849→}

   851→func TestInstallGitHook_IdempotentWithChaining(t *testing.T) {
   852→	_, hooksDir := initHooksTestRepo(t)
   853→
   854→	// Create a custom hook, then install
   855→	customHookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, "prepare-commit-msg")
   856→	if err := os.WriteFile(customHookPath, []byte("#!/bin/sh\necho custom\n"), 0o755); err != nil {
   857→		t.Fatalf("failed to create custom hook: %v", err)
   858→	}
   859→
   860→	firstCount, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   861→	if err != nil {
   862→		t.Fatalf("first InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   863→	}
   864→	if firstCount == 0 {
   865→		t.Error("first install should install hooks")
   866→	}
   867→
   868→	// Re-install should return 0 (idempotent)
   869→	secondCount, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   870→	if err != nil {
   871→		t.Fatalf("second InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   872→	}
   873→	if secondCount != 0 {
   874→		t.Errorf("second InstallGitHook() = %d, want 0 (idempotent)", secondCount)
   875→	}
   876→}
