   904→func TestInstallGitHook_MixedHooks(t *testing.T) {
   905→	_, hooksDir := initHooksTestRepo(t)
   906→
   907→	// Only create custom hooks for some hooks
   908→	customHooks := map[string]string{
   909→		"prepare-commit-msg": "#!/bin/sh\necho 'custom pcm'\n",
   910→		"pre-push":           "#!/bin/sh\necho 'custom prepush'\n",
   911→	}
   912→	for name, content := range customHooks {
   913→		hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, name)
   914→		if err := os.WriteFile(hookPath, []byte(content), 0o755); err != nil {
   915→			t.Fatalf("failed to create %s: %v", name, err)
   916→		}
   917→	}
   918→
   919→	_, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   920→	if err != nil {
   921→		t.Fatalf("InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   922→	}
   923→
   924→	// Hooks with pre-existing content should have backups and chain calls
   925→	for name := range customHooks {
   926→		backupPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, name+backupSuffix)
   927→		if _, err := os.Stat(backupPath); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   928→			t.Errorf("backup for %s should exist", name)
   929→		}
   930→
   931→		data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(hooksDir, name))
   932→		if err != nil {
   933→			t.Fatalf("hook %s should exist: %v", name, err)
   934→		}
   935→		if !strings.Contains(string(data), chainComment) {
   936→			t.Errorf("hook %s should contain chain call", name)
   937→		}
   938→	}
   939→
   940→	// Hooks without pre-existing content should NOT have backups or chain calls
   941→	noCustom := []string{"commit-msg", "post-commit"}
   942→	for _, name := range noCustom {
   943→		backupPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, name+backupSuffix)
   944→		if _, err := os.Stat(backupPath); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
   945→			t.Errorf("backup for %s should NOT exist", name)
   946→		}
   947→
   948→		data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(hooksDir, name))
   949→		if err != nil {
   950→			t.Fatalf("hook %s should exist: %v", name, err)
   951→		}
   952→		if strings.Contains(string(data), chainComment) {
   953→			t.Errorf("hook %s should NOT contain chain call", name)
   954→		}
   955→	}
   956→}

   958→func TestRemoveGitHook_RestoresBackup(t *testing.T) {
   959→	_, hooksDir := initHooksTestRepo(t)
   960→
   961→	// Create a custom hook, install (backs it up), then remove
   962→	customContent := "#!/bin/sh\necho 'my custom hook'\n"
   963→	hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, "prepare-commit-msg")
   964→	if err := os.WriteFile(hookPath, []byte(customContent), 0o755); err != nil {
   965→		t.Fatalf("failed to create custom hook: %v", err)
   966→	}
   967→
   968→	_, err := InstallGitHook(true)
   969→	if err != nil {
   970→		t.Fatalf("InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   971→	}
   972→
   973→	removed, err := RemoveGitHook()
   974→	if err != nil {
   975→		t.Fatalf("RemoveGitHook() error = %v", err)
   976→	}
   977→	if removed == 0 {
   978→		t.Error("RemoveGitHook() should remove hooks")
   979→	}
   980→
   981→	// Original custom hook should be restored
   982→	data, err := os.ReadFile(hookPath)
   983→	if err != nil {
   984→		t.Fatalf("hook should be restored: %v", err)
   985→	}
   986→	if string(data) != customContent {
   987→		t.Errorf("restored hook content = %q, want %q", string(data), customContent)
   988→	}
   989→
   990→	// Backup should be gone
   991→	backupPath := hookPath + backupSuffix
   992→	if _, err := os.Stat(backupPath); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
   993→		t.Error("backup should be removed after restore")
   994→	}
   995→}

   997→func TestRemoveGitHook_RestoresBackupWhenHookAlreadyGone(t *testing.T) {
   998→	_, hooksDir := initHooksTestRepo(t)
   999→
  1000→	// Create custom hook, install (creates backup), then delete the main hook
  1001→	customContent := "#!/bin/sh\necho 'original'\n"
  1002→	hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, "prepare-commit-msg")
  1003→	if err := os.WriteFile(hookPath, []byte(customContent), 0o755); err != nil {
  1004→		t.Fatalf("failed to create custom hook: %v", err)
  1005→	}
  1006→
  1007→	_, err := InstallGitHook(true)
  1008→	if err != nil {
  1009→		t.Fatalf("InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
  1010→	}
  1011→
  1012→	// Simulate another tool deleting our hook
  1013→	if err := os.Remove(hookPath); err != nil {
  1014→		t.Fatalf("failed to remove hook: %v", err)
  1015→	}
  1016→
  1017→	_, err = RemoveGitHook()
  1018→	if err != nil {
  1019→		t.Fatalf("RemoveGitHook() error = %v", err)
  1020→	}
  1021→
  1022→	// Backup should be restored even though the main hook was already gone
  1023→	data, err := os.ReadFile(hookPath)
  1024→	if err != nil {
  1025→		t.Fatal("backup should be restored to main hook path")
  1026→	}
  1027→	if string(data) != customContent {
  1028→		t.Errorf("restored hook content = %q, want %q", string(data), customContent)
  1029→	}
  1030→
  1031→	// Backup file should be gone
  1032→	backupPath := hookPath + backupSuffix
  1033→	if _, err := os.Stat(backupPath); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
  1034→		t.Error("backup file should not exist after restore")
  1035→	}
  1036→}

  1038→func TestGenerateChainedContent(t *testing.T) {
  1039→	t.Parallel()
  1040→
  1041→	base := "#!/bin/sh\n# Entire CLI hooks\nentire hooks git pre-push \"$1\" || true\n"
  1042→	result := generateChainedContent(base, "pre-push")
  1043→
  1044→	// Should start with the base content
  1045→	if !strings.HasPrefix(result, base) {
  1046→		t.Error("chained content should start with base content")
  1047→	}
  1048→
  1049→	// Should contain the chain comment
  1050→	if !strings.Contains(result, chainComment) {
  1051→		t.Error("chained content should contain chain comment")
  1052→	}
  1053→
  1054→	// Should resolve hook directory from $0
  1055→	if !strings.Contains(result, `_entire_hook_dir="$(dirname "$0")"`) {
  1056→		t.Error("chained content should resolve hook directory from $0")
  1057→	}
  1058→
  1059→	// Should check executable permission on backup
  1060→	expectedCheck := `[ -x "$_entire_hook_dir/pre-push` + backupSuffix + `" ]`
  1061→	if !strings.Contains(result, expectedCheck) {
  1062→		t.Errorf("chained content should check -x on backup, got:\n%s", result)
  1063→	}
  1064→
  1065→	// Should forward all arguments with "$@"
  1066→	expectedExec := `"$_entire_hook_dir/pre-push` + backupSuffix + `" "$@"`
  1067→	if !strings.Contains(result, expectedExec) {
  1068→		t.Errorf("chained content should execute backup with $@, got:\n%s", result)
  1069→	}
  1070→}
  1071→
  1072→func TestInstallGitHook_InstallRemoveReinstall(t *testing.T) {
  1073→	_, hooksDir := initHooksTestRepo(t)
  1074→
  1075→	// Create a custom hook
  1076→	customContent := "#!/bin/sh\necho 'user hook'\n"
  1077→	hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, "prepare-commit-msg")
  1078→	if err := os.WriteFile(hookPath, []byte(customContent), 0o755); err != nil {
  1079→		t.Fatalf("failed to create custom hook: %v", err)
  1080→	}
  1081→
  1082→	// Install: should back up and chain
  1083→	count, err := InstallGitHook(true)
  1084→	if err != nil {
  1085→		t.Fatalf("first install error: %v", err)
  1086→	}
  1087→	if count == 0 {
  1088→		t.Error("first install should install hooks")
  1089→	}
  1090→	backupPath := hookPath + backupSuffix
  1091→	if !fileExists(backupPath) {
  1092→		t.Fatal("backup should exist after install")
  1093→	}
  1094→
  1095→	// Remove: should restore backup
  1096→	_, err = RemoveGitHook()
  1097→	if err != nil {
  1098→		t.Fatalf("remove error: %v", err)
  1099→	}
  1100→	data, err := os.ReadFile(hookPath)
  1101→	if err != nil {
  1102→		t.Fatal("hook should be restored after remove")
  1103→	}
  1104→	if string(data) != customContent {
  1105→		t.Errorf("restored hook = %q, want %q", string(data), customContent)
  1106→	}
  1107→	if fileExists(backupPath) {
  1108→		t.Error("backup should not exist after remove")
  1109→	}
  1110→
  1111→	// Reinstall: should back up again and chain
  1112→	count, err = InstallGitHook(true)
  1113→	if err != nil {
  1114→		t.Fatalf("reinstall error: %v", err)
  1115→	}
  1116→	if count == 0 {
  1117→		t.Error("reinstall should install hooks")
  1118→	}
  1119→	if !fileExists(backupPath) {
  1120→		t.Fatal("backup should exist after reinstall")
  1121→	}
  1122→	data, err = os.ReadFile(hookPath)
  1123→	if err != nil {
  1124→		t.Fatal("hook should exist after reinstall")
  1125→	}

  1134→func TestRemoveGitHook_DoesNotOverwriteReplacedHook(t *testing.T) {
  1135→	_, hooksDir := initHooksTestRepo(t)
  1136→
  1137→	// User has custom hook A
  1138→	hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, "prepare-commit-msg")
  1139→	hookAContent := "#!/bin/sh\necho 'hook A'\n"
  1140→	if err := os.WriteFile(hookPath, []byte(hookAContent), 0o755); err != nil {
  1141→		t.Fatalf("failed to create hook A: %v", err)
  1142→	}
  1143→
  1144→	// entire enable: backs up A, installs our hook with chain
  1145→	_, err := InstallGitHook(true)
  1146→	if err != nil {
  1147→		t.Fatalf("InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
  1148→	}
  1149→
  1150→	// User replaces our hook with their own hook B
  1151→	hookBContent := "#!/bin/sh\necho 'hook B'\n"
  1152→	if err := os.WriteFile(hookPath, []byte(hookBContent), 0o755); err != nil {
  1153→		t.Fatalf("failed to create hook B: %v", err)
  1154→	}
  1155→
  1156→	// entire disable: should NOT overwrite hook B with backup A
  1157→	_, err = RemoveGitHook()
  1158→	if err != nil {
  1159→		t.Fatalf("RemoveGitHook() error = %v", err)
  1160→	}
  1161→
  1162→	// Hook B should still be in place
  1163→	data, err := os.ReadFile(hookPath)
  1164→	if err != nil {
  1165→		t.Fatal("hook should still exist")
  1166→	}
  1167→	if string(data) != hookBContent {
  1168→		t.Errorf("hook content = %q, want hook B %q (should not be overwritten by backup)", string(data), hookBContent)
  1169→	}
  1170→
  1171→	// Backup should still exist (not consumed)
  1172→	backupPath := hookPath + backupSuffix
  1173→	if !fileExists(backupPath) {
  1174→		t.Error("backup should be left in place when hook was modified")
  1175→	}
  1176→}
  1177→

  1179→	if os.Getuid() == 0 {

  1199→	})
  1200→
  1201→	// Remove hooks should now fail with permission error

{
  "file_path": "/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/hook_managers.go",
  "content": "package strategy\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths\"\n)\n\n// HookManager describes an external hook manager detected in a repository.\ntype HookManager struct {\n\tName            string // \"Husky\", \"Lefthook\", \"pre-commit\", \"Overcommit\"\n\tConfigPath      string // relative path that triggered detection (e.g., \".husky/\")\n\tOverwritesHooks bool   // true if the tool will overwrite Entire's hooks on reinstall\n}\n\n// DetectHookManagers checks the repository root for known hook manager config\n// files/directories. Detection is filesystem-only (os.Stat, no file reads).\nfunc DetectHookManagers(repoRoot string) []HookManager {\n\tvar managers []HookManager\n\n\tchecks := []struct {\n\t\tpath            string\n\t\tname            string\n\t\tconfigPath      string\n\t\toverwritesHooks bool\n\t}{\n\t\t{filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".husky\", \"_\"), \"Husky\", \".husky/\", true},\n\t\t{filepath.Join(repoRoot, \"lefthook.yml\"), \"Lefthook\", \"lefthook.yml\", false},\n\t\t{filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".lefthook.yml\"), \"Lefthook\", \".lefthook.yml\", false},\n\t\t{filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".pre-commit-config.yaml\"), \"pre-commit\", \".pre-commit-config.yaml\", false},\n\t\t{filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".overcommit.yml\"), \"Overcommit\", \".overcommit.yml\", false},\n\t}\n\n\tseen := make(map[string]bool)\n\tfor _, c := range checks {\n\t\tif _, err := os.Stat(c.path); err == nil {\n\t\t\tif seen[c.name] {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue // e.g., lefthook.yml and .lefthook.yml both present\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tseen[c.name] = true\n\t\t\tmanagers = append(managers, HookManager{\n\t\t\t\tName:            c.name,\n\t\t\t\tConfigPath:      c.configPath,\n\t\t\t\tOverwritesHooks: c.overwritesHooks,\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn managers\n}\n\n// HookManagerWarning builds a warning string for detected hook managers.\n// cmdPrefix is the CLI command prefix (e.g., \"entire\" or \"go run ./cmd/entire/main.go\").\nfunc HookManagerWarning(managers []HookManager, cmdPrefix string) string {\n\tif len(managers) == 0 {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\n\tvar b strings.Builder\n\n\tspecs := buildHookSpecs(cmdPrefix)\n\n\tfor _, m := range managers {\n\t\tif m.OverwritesHooks {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"Warning: %s detected (%s)\\n\", m.Name, m.ConfigPath)\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"\\n\")\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"  %s may overwrite hooks installed by Entire on npm install.\\n\", m.Name)\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"  To make Entire hooks permanent, add these lines to your %s hook files:\\n\", m.Name)\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"\\n\")\n\n\t\t\t// Extract the hook directory prefix from the config path.\n\t\t\t// e.g. \".husky/\" → \".husky/\"\n\t\t\thookDir := m.ConfigPath\n\n\t\t\tfor _, spec := range specs {\n\t\t\t\tcmdLine := extractCommandLine(spec.content)\n\t\t\t\tif cmdLine == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"    %s%s:\\n\", hookDir, spec.name)\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"      %s\\n\", cmdLine)\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"\\n\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"Note: %s detected (%s)\\n\", m.Name, m.ConfigPath)\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"\\n\")\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"  If %s reinstalls hooks, run 'entire enable' to restore Entire's hooks.\\n\", m.Name)\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"\\n\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn b.String()\n}\n\n// extractCommandLine returns the first non-shebang, non-comment, non-empty line\n// from a hook script. This is the actual command invocation line.\nfunc extractCommandLine(hookContent string) string {\n\tfor _, line := range strings.Split(hookContent, \"\\n\") {\n\t\ttrimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)\n\t\tif trimmed == \"\" || strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, \"#\") {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn trimmed\n\t}\n\treturn \"\"\n}\n\n// CheckAndWarnHookManagers detects external hook managers and writes a warning\n// to w if any are found. Returns the detected managers.\nfunc CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w io.Writer) []HookManager {\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tmanagers := DetectHookManagers(repoRoot)\n\tif len(managers) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tvar cmdPrefix string\n\tif isLocalDev() {\n\t\tcmdPrefix = \"go run ./cmd/entire/main.go\"\n\t} else {\n\t\tcmdPrefix = \"entire\"\n\t}\n\n\twarning := HookManagerWarning(managers, cmdPrefix)\n\tif warning != \"\" {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w)\n\t\tfmt.Fprint(w, warning)\n\t}\n\n\treturn managers\n}\n"
}

hook\",\n\t\t\tcontent: \"#!/bin/sh\\n# Entire CLI hooks\\nentire hooks git post-commit 2>/dev/null || true\\n\",\n\t\t\twant:    \"entire hooks git post-commit 2>/dev/null || true\",\n\t\t},\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:    \"multiple comments\",\n\t\t\tcontent: \"#!/bin/sh\\n# comment 1\\n# comment 2\\nentire hooks git pre-push \\\"$1\\\" || true\\n\",\n\t\t\twant:    `entire hooks git pre-push \"$1\" || true`,\n\t\t},\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:    \"empty content\",\n\t\t\tcontent: \"\",\n\t\t\twant:    \"\",\n\t\t},\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:    \"only comments\",\n\t\t\tcontent: \"#!/bin/sh\\n# just a comment\\n\",\n\t\t\twant:    \"\",\n\t\t},\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:    \"whitespace around command\",\n\t\t\tcontent: \"#!/bin/sh\\n# comment\\n  entire hooks git commit-msg \\\"$1\\\" || exit 1  \\n\",\n\t\t\twant:    `entire hooks git commit-msg \"$1\" || exit 1`,\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, tt := range tests {\n\t\tt.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tt.Parallel()\n\t\t\tgot := extractCommandLine(tt.content)\n\t\t\tif got != tt.want {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"extractCommandLine() = %q, want %q\", got, tt.want)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestCheckAndWarnHookManagers_NoManagers(t *testing.T) {\n\t// Needs t.Chdir, cannot be parallel\n\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\tt.Chdir(tmpDir)\n\n\t// Init a git repo so paths.RepoRoot() works\n\tinitGitRepo(t, tmpDir)\n\n\tvar buf bytes.Buffer\n\tmanagers := CheckAndWarnHookManagers(&buf)\n\n\tif len(managers) != 0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected 0 managers, got %d\", len(managers))\n\t}\n\tif buf.Len() != 0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected no output, got %q\", buf.String())\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestCheckAndWarnHookManagers_WithHusky(t *testing.T) {\n\t// Needs t.Chdir, cannot be parallel\n\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\tt.Chdir(tmpDir)\n\n\tinitGitRepo(t, tmpDir)\n\n\t// Create .husky/_/ directory\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(tmpDir, \".husky\", \"_\"), 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create .husky/_/: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tvar buf bytes.Buffer\n\tmanagers := CheckAndWarnHookManagers(&buf)\n\n\tif len(managers) != 1 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 1 manager, got %d\", len(managers))\n\t}\n\tif managers[0].Name != \"Husky\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected Husky, got %s\", managers[0].Name)\n\t}\n\n\toutput := buf.String()\n\tif !strings.Contains(output, \"Warning: Husky detected\") {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected warning output, got %q\", output)\n\t}\n}\n\n// initGitRepo initializes a git repo and clears the repo root cache.\nfunc initGitRepo(t *testing.T, dir string) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\n\tcmd := gitCommand(\"init\")\n\tcmd.Dir = dir\n\tif err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to init git repo: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tpaths.ClearRepoRootCache()\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

All tests pass, including the new ones. Let me do a quick summary of the changes.

## Summary

Created 2 new files and modified 1 existing file:

### New: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/hook_managers.go`
- **`HookManager` struct** — holds name, config path, and whether the tool overwrites hooks
- **`DetectHookManagers(repoRoot)`** — filesystem-only detection (os.Stat) for Husky v9+, Lefthook, pre-commit, and Overcommit. Deduplicates managers (e.g., `lefthook.yml` + `.lefthook.yml`).
- **`HookManagerWarning(managers, cmdPrefix)`** — generates warning text. For tools that overwrite hooks (Husky), shows copy-paste instructions extracted from `buildHookSpecs()` via `extractCommandLine()` to stay in sync with actual hook content. For other tools, shows a simpler "re-run `entire enable`" note.
- **`extractCommandLine(hookContent)`** — returns the first non-shebang, non-comment line from a hook script
- **`CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w)`** — public entry point called from setup.go; resolves repo root, detects managers, and writes warning

### New: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/hook_managers_test.go`
15 tests covering:
- Detection of each individual manager (Husky, Lefthook, pre-commit, Overcommit)
- Lefthook deduplication (both config filenames present)
- Multiple managers detected simultaneously
- Warning output for Husky (all 4 hook commands present)
- Warning output for category B (note, not warning)
- Empty/nil manager slices
- Local dev command prefix in warnings
- `extractCommandLine` with various inputs
- Integration tests for `CheckAndWarnHookManagers` (with/without Husky)

### Modified: `cmd/entire/cli/setup.go`
Added `strategy.CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w)` after each `InstallGitHook()` call in:
1. `runEnableWithStrategy()` (line ~309)
2. `runEnableInteractive()` (line ~408)
3. `setupAgentHooksNonInteractive()` (line ~618)
4. `setupGitHook()` (line ~720, uses `os.Stderr`)

> DEVELOPER

Lefthook looks for a configuration file using the pattern {.,}lefthook.{yml,yaml,json,toml} DeepWiki. So all valid main config files are:

lefthook.yml
lefthook.yaml
lefthook.json
lefthook.toml
.lefthook.yml
.lefthook.yaml
.lefthook.json
.lefthook.toml

Plus the local override variants (same pattern with -local):

lefthook-local.yml / .lefthook-local.yml (and .yaml, .json, .toml)

     1→package strategy
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"io"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"path/filepath"
     8→	"strings"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// HookManager describes an external hook manager detected in a repository.
    14→type HookManager struct {
    15→	Name            string // "Husky", "Lefthook", "pre-commit", "Overcommit"
    16→	ConfigPath      string // relative path that triggered detection (e.g., ".husky/")
    17→	OverwritesHooks bool   // true if the tool will overwrite Entire's hooks on reinstall
    18→}
    19→
    20→// DetectHookManagers checks the repository root for known hook manager config
    21→// files/directories. Detection is filesystem-only (os.Stat, no file reads).
    22→func DetectHookManagers(repoRoot string) []HookManager {
    23→	var managers []HookManager
    24→
    25→	checks := []struct {
    26→		path            string
    27→		name            string
    28→		configPath      string
    29→		overwritesHooks bool
    30→	}{
    31→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".husky", "_"), "Husky", ".husky/", true},
    32→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, "lefthook.yml"), "Lefthook", "lefthook.yml", false},
    33→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".lefthook.yml"), "Lefthook", ".lefthook.yml", false},
    34→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".pre-commit-config.yaml"), "pre-commit", ".pre-commit-config.yaml", false},
    35→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".overcommit.yml"), "Overcommit", ".overcommit.yml", false},
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
    39→	for _, c := range checks {
    40→		if _, err := os.Stat(c.path); err == nil {
    41→			if seen[c.name] {
    42→				continue // e.g., lefthook.yml and .lefthook.yml both present
    43→			}
    44→			seen[c.name] = true
    45→			managers = append(managers, HookManager{
    46→				Name:            c.name,
    47→				ConfigPath:      c.configPath,
    48→				OverwritesHooks: c.overwritesHooks,
    49→			})
    50→		}
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	return managers
    54→}
    55→
    56→// HookManagerWarning builds a warning string for detected hook managers.
    57→// cmdPrefix is the CLI command prefix (e.g., "entire" or "go run ./cmd/entire/main.go").
    58→func HookManagerWarning(managers []HookManager, cmdPrefix string) string {
    59→	if len(managers) == 0 {
    60→		return ""
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	var b strings.Builder
    64→
    65→	specs := buildHookSpecs(cmdPrefix)
    66→
    67→	for _, m := range managers {
    68→		if m.OverwritesHooks {
    69→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "Warning: %s detected (%s)\n", m.Name, m.ConfigPath)
    70→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "\n")
    71→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "  %s may overwrite hooks installed by Entire on npm install.\n", m.Name)
    72→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "  To make Entire hooks permanent, add these lines to your %s hook files:\n", m.Name)
    73→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "\n")
    74→
    75→			// Extract the hook directory prefix from the config path.
    76→			// e.g. ".husky/" → ".husky/"
    77→			hookDir := m.ConfigPath
    78→
    79→			for _, spec := range specs {
    80→				cmdLine := extractCommandLine(spec.content)
    81→				if cmdLine == "" {
    82→					continue
    83→				}
    84→				fmt.Fprintf(&b, "    %s%s:\n", hookDir, spec.name)
    85→				fmt.Fprintf(&b, "      %s\n", cmdLine)
    86→				fmt.Fprintf(&b, "\n")
    87→			}
    88→		} else {
    89→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "Note: %s detected (%s)\n", m.Name, m.ConfigPath)
    90→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "\n")
    91→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "  If %s reinstalls hooks, run 'entire enable' to restore Entire's hooks.\n", m.Name)
    92→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "\n")
    93→		}
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	return b.String()
    97→}
    98→
    99→// extractCommandLine returns the first non-shebang, non-comment, non-empty line
   100→// from a hook script. This is the actual command invocation line.
   101→func extractCommandLine(hookContent string) string {
   102→	for _, line := range strings.Split(hookContent, "\n") {
   103→		trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)
   104→		if trimmed == "" || strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "#") {
   105→			continue
   106→		}
   107→		return trimmed
   108→	}
   109→	return ""
   110→}
   111→
   112→// CheckAndWarnHookManagers detects external hook managers and writes a warning
   113→// to w if any are found. Returns the detected managers.
   114→func CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w io.Writer) []HookManager {
   115→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   116→	if err != nil {
   117→		return nil
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	managers := DetectHookManagers(repoRoot)
   121→	if len(managers) == 0 {
   122→		return nil
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	var cmdPrefix string
   126→	if isLocalDev() {
   127→		cmdPrefix = "go run ./cmd/entire/main.go"
   128→	} else {
   129→		cmdPrefix = "entire"
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	warning := HookManagerWarning(managers, cmdPrefix)
   133→	if warning != "" {
   134→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
   135→		fmt.Fprint(w, warning)
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	return managers
   139→}
   140→
```

     1→package strategy

     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"

    12→
    13→// HookManager describes an external hook manager detected in a repository.
    14→type HookManager struct {
    15→	Name            string // "Husky", "Lefthook", "pre-commit", "Overcommit"
    16→	ConfigPath      string // relative path that triggered detection (e.g., ".husky/")
    17→	OverwritesHooks bool   // true if the tool will overwrite Entire's hooks on reinstall
    18→}
    19→
    20→// DetectHookManagers checks the repository root for known hook manager config
    21→// files/directories. Detection is filesystem-only (os.Stat, no file reads).
    22→func DetectHookManagers(repoRoot string) []HookManager {
    23→	var managers []HookManager
    24→
    25→	checks := []struct {
    26→		path            string
    27→		name            string
    28→		configPath      string
    29→		overwritesHooks bool
    30→	}{
    31→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".husky", "_"), "Husky", ".husky/", true},
    32→
    33→		// Lefthook supports {.,}lefthook{,-local}.{yml,yaml,json,toml}
    34→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, "lefthook.yml"), "Lefthook", "lefthook.yml", false},
    35→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, "lefthook.yaml"), "Lefthook", "lefthook.yaml", false},
    36→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, "lefthook.json"), "Lefthook", "lefthook.json", false},
    37→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, "lefthook.toml"), "Lefthook", "lefthook.toml", false},
    38→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".lefthook.yml"), "Lefthook", ".lefthook.yml", false},
    39→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".lefthook.yaml"), "Lefthook", ".lefthook.yaml", false},
    40→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".lefthook.json"), "Lefthook", ".lefthook.json", false},
    41→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".lefthook.toml"), "Lefthook", ".lefthook.toml", false},
    42→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, "lefthook-local.yml"), "Lefthook", "lefthook-local.yml", false},
    43→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, "lefthook-local.yaml"), "Lefthook", "lefthook-local.yaml", false},
    44→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, "lefthook-local.json"), "Lefthook", "lefthook-local.json", false},
    45→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, "lefthook-local.toml"), "Lefthook", "lefthook-local.toml", false},
    46→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".lefthook-local.yml"), "Lefthook", ".lefthook-local.yml", false},
    47→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".lefthook-local.yaml"), "Lefthook", ".lefthook-local.yaml", false},
    48→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".lefthook-local.json"), "Lefthook", ".lefthook-local.json", false},
    49→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".lefthook-local.toml"), "Lefthook", ".lefthook-local.toml", false},
    50→
    51→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".pre-commit-config.yaml"), "pre-commit", ".pre-commit-config.yaml", false},
    52→		{filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".overcommit.yml"), "Overcommit", ".overcommit.yml", false},
    53→	}
    54→
    55→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
    56→	for _, c := range checks {
    57→		if _, err := os.Stat(c.path); err == nil {
    58→			if seen[c.name] {
    59→				continue // e.g., lefthook.yml and .lefthook.yml both present
    60→			}
    61→			seen[c.name] = true
    62→			managers = append(managers, HookManager{
    63→				Name:            c.name,
    64→				ConfigPath:      c.configPath,
    65→				OverwritesHooks: c.overwritesHooks,
    66→			})
    67→		}
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	return managers
    71→}
    72→
    73→// HookManagerWarning builds a warning string for detected hook managers.
    74→// cmdPrefix is the CLI command prefix (e.g., "entire" or "go run ./cmd/entire/main.go").
    75→func HookManagerWarning(managers []HookManager, cmdPrefix string) string {
    76→	if len(managers) == 0 {
    77→		return ""
    78→	}
    79→
    80→	var b strings.Builder
    81→
    82→	specs := buildHookSpecs(cmdPrefix)
    83→
    84→	for _, m := range managers {
    85→		if m.OverwritesHooks {
    86→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "Warning: %s detected (%s)\n", m.Name, m.ConfigPath)
    87→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "\n")
    88→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "  %s may overwrite hooks installed by Entire on npm install.\n", m.Name)
    89→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "  To make Entire hooks permanent, add these lines to your %s hook files:\n", m.Name)
    90→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "\n")
    91→
    92→			// Extract the hook directory prefix from the config path.
    93→			// e.g. ".husky/" → ".husky/"
    94→			hookDir := m.ConfigPath
    95→
    96→			for _, spec := range specs {
    97→				cmdLine := extractCommandLine(spec.content)
    98→				if cmdLine == "" {
    99→					continue
   100→				}
   101→				fmt.Fprintf(&b, "    %s%s:\n", hookDir, spec.name)
   102→				fmt.Fprintf(&b, "      %s\n", cmdLine)
   103→				fmt.Fprintf(&b, "\n")
   104→			}
   105→		} else {
   106→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "Note: %s detected (%s)\n", m.Name, m.ConfigPath)
   107→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "\n")
   108→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "  If %s reinstalls hooks, run 'entire enable' to restore Entire's hooks.\n", m.Name)
   109→			fmt.Fprintf(&b, "\n")
   110→		}
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	return b.String()
   114→}
   115→
   116→// extractCommandLine returns the first non-shebang, non-comment, non-empty line
   117→// from a hook script. This is the actual command invocation line.
   118→func extractCommandLine(hookContent string) string {
   119→	for _, line := range strings.Split(hookContent, "\n") {
   120→		trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)
   121→		if trimmed == "" || strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "#") {
   122→			continue
   123→		}
   124→		return trimmed
   125→	}
   126→	return ""
   127→}
   128→
   129→// CheckAndWarnHookManagers detects external hook managers and writes a warning
   130→// to w if any are found. Returns the detected managers.
   131→func CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w io.Writer) []HookManager {
   132→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   133→	if err != nil {
   134→		return nil
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	managers := DetectHookManagers(repoRoot)
   138→	if len(managers) == 0 {
   139→		return nil
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	var cmdPrefix string
   143→	if isLocalDev() {
   144→		cmdPrefix = "go run ./cmd/entire/main.go"
   145→	} else {
   146→		cmdPrefix = "entire"
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	warning := HookManagerWarning(managers, cmdPrefix)
   150→	if warning != "" {
   151→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
   152→		fmt.Fprint(w, warning)
   153→	}
   154→
   155→	return managers
   156→}
   157→

   204→func TestHookManagerWarning_Husky(t *testing.T) {
   205→	t.Parallel()
   206→
   207→	managers := []HookManager{
   208→		{Name: "Husky", ConfigPath: ".husky/", OverwritesHooks: true},
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	warning := HookManagerWarning(managers, "entire")
   212→
   213→	// Should contain all 4 hook file references
   214→	for _, hook := range gitHookNames {
   215→		if !strings.Contains(warning, ".husky/"+hook+":") {
   216→			t.Errorf("warning should contain .husky/%s:", hook)
   217→		}
   218→	}
   219→
   220→	// Should contain the actual command lines from buildHookSpecs
   221→	specs := buildHookSpecs("entire")
   222→	for _, spec := range specs {
   223→		cmdLine := extractCommandLine(spec.content)
   224→		if cmdLine == "" {
   225→			t.Errorf("failed to extract command line for %s", spec.name)
   226→			continue
   227→		}
   228→		if !strings.Contains(warning, cmdLine) {
   229→			t.Errorf("warning should contain command line %q for %s", cmdLine, spec.name)
   230→		}
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	// Should mention Husky by name and warn about overwriting
   234→	if !strings.Contains(warning, "Warning: Husky detected") {
   235→		t.Error("warning should start with 'Warning: Husky detected'")
   236→	}
   237→	if !strings.Contains(warning, "may overwrite hooks") {
   238→		t.Error("warning should mention 'may overwrite hooks'")
   239→	}
   240→}

   312→func TestExtractCommandLine(t *testing.T) {
   313→	t.Parallel()
   314→
   315→	tests := []struct {
   316→		name    string
   317→		content string
   318→		want    string
   319→	}{
   320→		{
   321→			name:    "standard hook",
   322→			content: "#!/bin/sh\n# Entire CLI hooks\nentire hooks git post-commit 2>/dev/null || true\n",
   323→			want:    "entire hooks git post-commit 2>/dev/null || true",
   324→		},
   325→		{
   326→			name:    "multiple comments",
   327→			content: "#!/bin/sh\n# comment 1\n# comment 2\nentire hooks git pre-push \"$1\" || true\n",
   328→			want:    `entire hooks git pre-push "$1" || true`,
   329→		},
   330→		{
   331→			name:    "empty content",
   332→			content: "",
   333→			want:    "",
   334→		},
   335→		{
   336→			name:    "only comments",
   337→			content: "#!/bin/sh\n# just a comment\n",
   338→			want:    "",
   339→		},
   340→		{
   341→			name:    "whitespace around command",
   342→			content: "#!/bin/sh\n# comment\n  entire hooks git commit-msg \"$1\" || exit 1  \n",
   343→			want:    `entire hooks git commit-msg "$1" || exit 1`,
   344→		},
   345→	}
   346→
   347→	for _, tt := range tests {
   348→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   349→			t.Parallel()
   350→			got := extractCommandLine(tt.content)
   351→			if got != tt.want {
   352→				t.Errorf("extractCommandLine() = %q, want %q", got, tt.want)
   353→			}
   354→		})
   355→	}
   356→}

   358→func TestCheckAndWarnHookManagers_NoManagers(t *testing.T) {
   359→	// Needs t.Chdir, cannot be parallel
   360→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   361→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
   362→
   363→	// Init a git repo so paths.RepoRoot() works
   364→	initGitRepo(t, tmpDir)
   365→
   366→	var buf bytes.Buffer
   367→	managers := CheckAndWarnHookManagers(&buf)
   368→
   369→	if len(managers) != 0 {

   405→// initGitRepo initializes a git repo and clears the repo root cache.
   406→func initGitRepo(t *testing.T, dir string) {
   407→	t.Helper()
   408→
   409→	ctx := context.Background()
   410→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "init")
   411→	cmd.Dir = dir
   412→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   413→		t.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   414→	}
   415→
   416→	paths.ClearRepoRootCache()
   417→}

Returns the detected managers.\nfunc CheckAndWarnHookManagers(w io.Writer) []hookManager {\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tmanagers := detectHookManagers(repoRoot)\n\tif len(managers) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tvar cmdPrefix string\n\tif isLocalDev() {\n\t\tcmdPrefix = \"go run ./cmd/entire/main.go\"\n\t} else {\n\t\tcmdPrefix = \"entire\"\n\t}\n\n\twarning := hookManagerWarning(managers, cmdPrefix)\n\tif warning != \"\" {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w)\n\t\tfmt.Fprint(w, warning)\n\t}\n\n\treturn managers\n}",

}

> AGENT

Done. `hookManager`, `detectHookManagers`, and `hookManagerWarning` are now unexported. Only `CheckAndWarnHookManagers` remains exported (needed by the `cli` package in `setup.go`), and its return type is now `void`.

> DEVELOPER

efthook is the most flexible in terms of config file naming. During installation, Lefthook looks for a configuration file using the pattern {.,}lefthook.{yml,yaml,json,toml} DeepWiki. So all valid main config files are:

lefthook.yml
lefthook.yaml
lefthook.json
lefthook.toml
.lefthook.yml
.lefthook.yaml
.lefthook.json
.lefthook.toml

Plus the local override variants (same pattern with -local):

lefthook-local.yml / .lefthook-local.yml (and .yaml, .json, .toml)

Here is the review summary:

---

## Review Complete

**Verdict: APPROVE** (with suggestions)

The review has been written to `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/docs/requirements/detect-hook-managers/review-01.md`.

### Key Findings

**Important (1 actionable item):**

The most significant issue is that **Husky detection targets `.husky/_`** (line 31 of `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/hook_managers.go`), which was a Husky v5-v8 internal directory. Husky v9+ places hooks directly in `.husky/` without the `_` subdirectory. This means the detection will miss Husky v9 projects. The fix is straightforward -- detect `.husky/` instead of `.husky/_`.

**No Critical issues found.**

### What Was Done Well

- **Clean architecture**: Detection, formatting, and orchestration are properly separated into testable functions (`detectHookManagers`, `hookManagerWarning`, `CheckAndWarnHookManagers`).
- **Thorough test coverage**: 17 test functions covering all managers, deduplication, multiple managers, both warning categories, empty input, and end-to-end integration.
- **Correct Lefthook coverage**: All 16 config file permutations `{.,}lefthook{,-local}.{yml,yaml,json,toml}` are generated.
- **Non-blocking design**: Detection failures silently return, never breaking the `entire enable` flow.
- **Consistent integration**: All 4 call sites in `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/setup.go` follow the same pattern -- called after `InstallGitHook`, before the success message.

### Minor Suggestions

- Move `buildHookSpecs` call inside the `OverwritesHooks` branch to avoid unnecessary allocation.
- Extract the anonymous struct used for Lefthook checks into a named type for readability.
- Consider reusing `initTestRepo` from `common_test.go` instead of adding a new `initGitRepo` helper.

Tests all pass and lint is clean.

## Review Summary

**APPROVE** with one important finding and a few minor suggestions:

### Important: Husky v9 detection
The detection checks for `.husky/_/` which is Husky v5-v8. Husky v9+ places hooks directly in `.husky/` without the `_` subdirectory. Should we update to detect `.husky/` instead?

### Minor suggestions
1. Move `buildHookSpecs` call inside the `OverwritesHooks` branch (avoids allocation when not needed)
2. Consider reusing existing `initTestRepo` helper instead of the new `initGitRepo`

Want me to apply the Husky v9 fix and the minor suggestions?
